```
636	
637	# ── Async batch prefetcher ─────────────────────────────────────────────
638	
639	class _BatchPrefetcher:
640	    """Background-thread batch builder so disk IO overlaps GPU compute.
641	
642	    The worker calls ``build_one_batch_fn()`` to produce one optimizer-microbatch
643	    (already on the target device) and pushes it to a bounded queue. The
644	    training main thread pulls via ``next_batch()``; if the worker had
645	    completed the batch ahead of time the call is near-instant.
646	
647	    Single-process, single CUDA context — torch.Tensor producer/consumer
648	    across two threads is safe so long as both share the default stream
649	    (we never touch streams here).
650	
651	    On any per-batch exception, the worker pushes a sentinel so the main
652	    loop can ``continue`` to the next microbatch instead of dying. The
653	    underlying error is logged via ``on_error``.
654	    """
655	    _SKIP = object()
656	
657	    def __init__(self, build_one_batch_fn, queue_size: int = 2,
658	                 on_error=None):
659	        self._fn = build_one_batch_fn
660	        self._on_error = on_error
661	        self._q: "_queue.Queue" = _queue.Queue(maxsize=queue_size)
662	        self._stop = threading.Event()
663	        self._th = threading.Thread(target=self._worker, name="batch-prefetcher", daemon=True)
664	        self._th.start()
665	
666	    def _worker(self):
667	        while not self._stop.is_set():
668	            try:
669	                batch = self._fn()
670	            except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
671	                if self._on_error is not None:
672	                    try:
673	                        self._on_error(exc)
674	                    except Exception:
675	                        pass
676	                batch = self._SKIP
677	            # bounded put; honour stop in case main is shutting down
678	            while not self._stop.is_set():
679	                try:
680	                    self._q.put(batch, timeout=1.0)
681	                    break
682	                except _queue.Full:
683	                    continue
684	
685	    def next_batch(self):
686	        """Blocking pull. Returns the batch dict or None on per-batch failure."""
687	        item = self._q.get()
688	        if item is self._SKIP:
689	            return None
690	        return item
691	
692	    def close(self):
693	        self._stop.set()
694	        # drain so worker can exit
695	        try:
696	            while True:
697	                self._q.get_nowait()
698	        except _queue.Empty:
699	            pass
700	        self._th.join(timeout=2.0)
701	
702	
703	def main():
704	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
705	    ap.add_argument("--smoke", action="store_true", help="short smoke train")
706	    ap.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=5000)
707	    ap.add_argument("--eval_every", type=int, default=250)
708	    ap.add_argument("--data_dir", default=str(DATA_DIR))
709	    ap.add_argument("--val_ind_dir", default=str(VAL_IND_DIR))
710	    ap.add_argument("--val_ood_dir", default=str(VAL_OOD_DIR))
711	    ap.add_argument("--vocab_cache", default=str(VOCAB_CACHE_V4))
712	    ap.add_argument("--output_dir", default=str(OUTPUT_DIR))
713	    ap.add_argument("--seq_len", type=int, default=SEQ_LEN_MAX)
714	    ap.add_argument("--eval_max_ind", type=int, default=200)
715	    ap.add_argument("--eval_max_ood", type=int, default=64)
716	    ap.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=SEED)
717	    ap.add_argument("--resume", default="", help="resume model weights from best.pt")
718	    ap.add_argument("--empty_cache_every", type=int, default=25,
719	                    help="release PyTorch CUDA cache every N optimizer steps; 0 disables")
720	    args = ap.parse_args()
721	    if args.smoke:
722	        args.steps = min(args.steps, 4)

852	        f"{LR_MIN_FACTOR:.0%} of LR over {args.steps - warmup_steps} step "
853	        f"(LR={LR:g}, end_LR={LR*LR_MIN_FACTOR:g})"
854	    )
855	
856	    def lr_for_step(step: int) -> float:
857	        """Linear warmup → cosine decay.
858	
859	        warmup: LR * (step / warmup_steps)
860	        post-warmup: LR * (LR_MIN_FACTOR + (1 - LR_MIN_FACTOR) * 0.5 *
861	                           (1 + cos(pi * progress)))
862	        progress = (step - warmup_steps) / (total_steps - warmup_steps)
863	        """
864	        if step < warmup_steps:
865	            return LR * step / max(1, warmup_steps)
866	        total = max(args.steps, warmup_steps + 1)
867	        progress = (step - warmup_steps) / max(1, total - warmup_steps)
868	        progress = min(1.0, max(0.0, progress))
869	        cos_factor = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.cos(math.pi * progress))
870	        return LR * (LR_MIN_FACTOR + (1.0 - LR_MIN_FACTOR) * cos_factor)
```

```
1	"""Sequence packing for SALA EAGLE-3 draft training.
2	
3	Pack multiple variable-length samples into one fixed-budget sequence to
4	eliminate padding waste. Each segment is fully isolated:
5	
6	  * Per-segment ``document_ids`` (0, 1, 2, ...). Cross-doc attention mask in
7	    the trainer blocks attention across segment boundaries.
8	  * Per-segment AOI sink + offset, so each segment's RoPE positions look
9	    independent (no global anchor on a packed sequence).
10	  * Per-segment tail masking. The trainer's TTT step k>0 left-shifts inputs
11	    by ``k`` positions, which on packed sequences pulls tokens from the
12	    next segment into the current segment's tail. We zero out the
13	    ``assistant_mask`` for the last ``ttt_steps`` positions of each segment
14	    so those polluted positions do not contribute to the loss.
15	
16	Public surface:
17	    index_lengths(data_dir, cache_path, force=False)
18	    pack_samples(samples, max_len, min_seg_len=64)
19	    aoi_position_ids_packed(seq_len, batch_size, document_ids, device,
20	                            training, aoi_cap, aoi_max_sink)
21	    mask_segment_tail(assistant_mask, document_ids, n_pad)
22	    make_packed_batch(samples, max_len, ttt_steps, device, dtype, min_seg_len=64)
23	    PackedFileSampler(files, lengths, max_len, min_seg_len)
24	"""
25	from __future__ import annotations
26	
27	import json
28	import multiprocessing as mp
29	import os
30	import random
31	import time
32	from pathlib import Path
33	
34	import torch
35	
36	
37	# ── Length index ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
38	
39	def _index_one(path_str: str) -> tuple[str, int | None, str | None]:
40	    """Single-file worker: returns (name, n_tokens, error_or_None).
41	
42	    Defined at module top level so multiprocessing.Pool can pickle it.
43	    """
44	    p = Path(path_str)
45	    try:
46	        d = torch.load(p, weights_only=True, map_location="cpu")
47	        return p.name, int(d["token_ids"].shape[0]), None
48	    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
49	        return p.name, None, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"
50	
51	
52	def index_lengths(data_dir: Path, cache_path: Path,
53	                  force: bool = False, verbose: bool = True,
54	                  num_workers: int | None = None) -> dict[str, int]:
55	    """Build {filename: token_ids length} index, persisted as JSON.
56	
57	    Loads every .pt once and records its ``token_ids`` length. Uses a
58	    multiprocessing pool to parallelize the torch.load — single-process
59	    runs at ~50 file/s due to I/O, multi-process scales linearly until
60	    storage saturates. Subsequent calls short-circuit on the cache; new
61	    files get filled in incrementally.
62	
63	    num_workers default = min(32, os.cpu_count() // 2). The bottleneck is
64	    storage IOPS, not CPU; oversubscribing has no benefit.
65	    """
66	    cache_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
67	    cached: dict[str, int] = {}
68	    if cache_path.exists() and not force:
69	        try:
70	            cached = json.loads(cache_path.read_text())
71	        except Exception:
72	            cached = {}
73	
74	    files = sorted(data_dir.glob("*.pt"))
75	    missing = [f for f in files if f.name not in cached]
76	    if not missing:
77	        return cached
78	
79	    if num_workers is None:
80	        num_workers = min(32, max(2, (os.cpu_count() or 4) // 2))
81	
82	    if verbose:
83	        print(f"[packing.index_lengths] {data_dir}: {len(missing)} new files to scan "
84	              f"({len(cached)} cached, {len(files)} total, workers={num_workers})")
85	    t0 = time.time()
86	    n_done = 0
87	    n_skip = 0
88	
89	    # fork is fine here: workers only do torch.load(map_location="cpu") and
90	    # never touch CUDA, so fork-time CUDA state inheritance is harmless. spawn
91	    # would re-import the parent's __main__ (which tries to rebuild the full
92	    # model + optimizer in every worker on every TTT step — many minutes lost
93	    # before the first index even completes).
94	    ctx = mp.get_context("fork")
95	    chunksize = max(1, len(missing) // (num_workers * 16) or 1)
96	    paths = [str(f) for f in missing]
97	
98	    with ctx.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:
99	        for name, n, err in pool.imap_unordered(_index_one, paths, chunksize=chunksize):
100	            n_done += 1
101	            if n is None:
102	                n_skip += 1
103	                if verbose:
104	                    print(f"  skip {name}: {err}")
105	                continue
106	            cached[name] = n
107	            if verbose and n_done % 1000 == 0:
108	                rate = n_done / (time.time() - t0)
109	                print(f"  scanned {n_done}/{len(missing)} ({time.time()-t0:.1f}s, {rate:.0f}/s)")
110	
111	    cache_path.write_text(json.dumps(cached))
112	    if verbose:
113	        print(f"[packing.index_lengths] wrote {cache_path} ({len(cached)} entries, "
114	              f"{n_skip} skipped, {time.time()-t0:.1f}s)")
115	    return cached
116	
117	
118	# ── Pack one bin from a list of loaded samples ─────────────────────────
119	
120	PACK_KEYS = (
121	    "token_ids",
122	    "aux_hidden",
123	    "top_logit_values",
124	    "top_logit_indices",
125	    "target_logsumexp",
126	    "assistant_mask",
127	)
128	
129	
130	def pack_samples(samples: list[dict], max_len: int,
131	                 min_seg_len: int = 64) -> dict | None:
132	    """Concatenate already-loaded samples along the seq axis.
133	
134	    Returns one packed dict (single-row, no batch dim) or None if no
135	    segment is admissible. Segments shorter than ``min_seg_len`` are
136	    silently dropped (they cannot afford the TTT tail masking).
137	
138	    Stops adding segments once the running length would exceed ``max_len``.
139	    The caller (bin packer) is responsible for choosing which samples to
140	    feed in; this routine does not reorder.
141	    """
142	    pieces = []
143	    total = 0
144	    for s in samples:
145	        n = int(s["token_ids"].shape[0])
146	        if n < min_seg_len:
147	            continue
148	        if total + n > max_len:
149	            continue
150	        pieces.append((s, n))
151	        total += n
152	    if not pieces:
153	        return None
154	
155	    out: dict = {}
156	    for k in PACK_KEYS:
157	        out[k] = torch.cat([s[k] for s, _ in pieces], dim=0)
158	
159	    seg_pieces = []
160	    for seg_idx, (_, n) in enumerate(pieces):
161	        seg_pieces.append(torch.full((n,), seg_idx, dtype=torch.int32))
162	    out["document_ids"] = torch.cat(seg_pieces, dim=0)
163	    out["_segment_lengths"] = [n for _, n in pieces]
164	    return out
165	
166	
167	# ── AOI per-segment positions ──────────────────────────────────────────
168	
169	def aoi_position_ids_packed(seq_len: int, batch_size: int,
170	                            document_ids: torch.Tensor,
171	                            device, training: bool,
172	                            aoi_cap: int, aoi_max_sink: int) -> torch.Tensor:
173	    """LongSpec AOI extended to packed sequences.
174	
175	    Each segment (run of equal ``document_ids[b]``) is treated as if it were
176	    a standalone shard:
177	        sink ~ U[0, aoi_max_sink]
178	        offset ~ U[0, aoi_cap - seg_len]
179	        pos = arange(seg_len), pos[sink:] += offset
180	    In eval / non-training mode positions are simply 0..S-1 globally.
181	    """
182	    if not training or seq_len >= aoi_cap:
183	        return (torch.arange(seq_len, device=device)
184	                .unsqueeze(0).expand(batch_size, -1).contiguous())
185	
186	    out = torch.zeros(batch_size, seq_len, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
187	    for b in range(batch_size):
188	        doc_row = document_ids[b]
189	        # Boundaries: indices where the segment starts.
190	        change = (doc_row[1:] != doc_row[:-1]).nonzero(as_tuple=True)[0]
191	        starts = [0] + (change + 1).tolist() + [seq_len]
192	        for i in range(len(starts) - 1):
193	            seg_start = starts[i]
194	            seg_end = starts[i + 1]
195	            seg_len = seg_end - seg_start
196	            if seg_len <= 0:
197	                continue
198	            sink = random.randint(0, aoi_max_sink)
199	            offset_max = max(0, aoi_cap - seg_len)
200	            offset = random.randint(0, offset_max) if offset_max > 0 else 0
201	            pos = torch.arange(seg_len, device=device)
202	            if sink < seg_len:
203	                pos[sink:] = pos[sink:] + offset
204	            out[b, seg_start:seg_end] = pos
205	    return out
206	
207	
208	# ── TTT tail mask ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
209	
210	def mask_segment_tail(assistant_mask: torch.Tensor,
211	                      document_ids: torch.Tensor,
212	                      n_pad: int) -> torch.Tensor:
213	    """Zero out the last ``n_pad`` positions of each segment.
214	
215	    A position p is "safe" iff for every k in [1, n_pad]:
216	        p + k is in-bounds AND document_ids[p + k] == document_ids[p].
217	    Otherwise after TTT left-shift by k the input at position p originates
218	    from a different segment (or out of bounds), so its supervision is
219	    polluted and we must drop it from the loss.
220	    """
221	    if n_pad <= 0:
222	        return assistant_mask
223	    am = assistant_mask.bool().clone() if assistant_mask.dtype != torch.bool \
224	        else assistant_mask.clone()
225	    B, S = document_ids.shape
226	    for k in range(1, n_pad + 1):
227	        valid = S - k
228	        if valid > 0:
229	            same = document_ids[:, :valid] == document_ids[:, k:k + valid]
230	            am[:, :valid] = am[:, :valid] & same
231	        # Positions in [S-k, S) cannot satisfy the "p+k in range" condition.
232	        if valid < S:
233	            am[:, max(valid, 0):S] = False
234	    return am
235	
236	
237	# ── Bin-packing dataset wrapper ────────────────────────────────────────
238	
239	class PackedFileSampler:
240	    """First-fit-decreasing bin packer over a static file list.
241	
242	    Each call to ``next_bin()`` returns a list of file paths whose
243	    ``token_ids`` lengths fit a single packed sequence of ``max_len``.
244	    Files shorter than ``min_seg_len`` are dropped at index time.
245	
246	    Internally the sampler keeps a shuffled list of (path, length) pairs
247	    and consumes them in FFD greedy order. When the pool empties it
248	    reshuffles. This is randomized but reproducible across epochs.
249	    """
250	
251	    def __init__(self, files: list[Path], lengths: dict[str, int],
252	                 max_len: int, min_seg_len: int = 64,
253	                 ttt_pad: int = 0, rng: random.Random | None = None):
254	        self.max_len = max_len
255	        self.min_seg_len = min_seg_len
256	        self.ttt_pad = ttt_pad
257	        self.rng = rng or random
258	        self._all = [
259	            (p, int(lengths[p.name]))
260	            for p in files
261	            if p.name in lengths and int(lengths[p.name]) >= min_seg_len
262	        ]
263	        if not self._all:
264	            raise RuntimeError("PackedFileSampler: no files >= min_seg_len after indexing")
265	        self._reset_pool()
266	
267	    def _reset_pool(self):
268	        pool = list(self._all)
269	        self.rng.shuffle(pool)
270	        # Sort by length descending — FFD prefers placing the largest first.
271	        pool.sort(key=lambda x: -x[1])
272	        self._pool = pool
273	
274	    def stats(self) -> dict:
275	        lens = [n for _, n in self._all]
276	        return {
277	            "n_files": len(self._all),
278	            "min_len": min(lens),
279	            "max_len": max(lens),
280	            "mean_len": sum(lens) / len(lens),
281	            "pool_remaining": len(self._pool),
282	        }
283	
284	    def next_bin(self) -> list[Path]:
285	        """Return file paths for one packed batch. Reshuffles when the pool
286	        empties without ever returning an empty bin (raises only if no
287	        single file fits — which would mean min_seg_len > max_len).
288	        """
289	        bin_files: list[Path] = []
290	        bin_filled = 0
291	        # Walk the pool once looking for files that fit.
292	        kept_indices: list[int] = []
293	        for i, (p, n) in enumerate(self._pool):
294	            if bin_filled + n <= self.max_len:
295	                bin_files.append(p)
296	                bin_filled += n
297	            else:
298	                kept_indices.append(i)
299	        # Compute the survivors (everything we didn't consume).
300	        new_pool = [self._pool[i] for i in kept_indices]
```

```
300	        new_pool = [self._pool[i] for i in kept_indices]
301	        # If the pool actually became smaller, this round made progress.
302	        if len(new_pool) < len(self._pool):
303	            self._pool = new_pool
304	        else:
305	            # Nothing fit (e.g. all remaining files exceed max_len).
306	            # Drop the first (largest leftover) so we don't loop.
307	            self._pool = self._pool[1:]
308	        if not self._pool:
309	            self._reset_pool()
310	        if not bin_files:
311	            # Single file exceeds max_len: take the smallest one anyway.
312	            small = min(self._all, key=lambda x: x[1])
313	            bin_files = [small[0]]
314	        return bin_files
315	
316	
317	# ── Top-level batch builder ────────────────────────────────────────────
318	
319	def make_packed_batch(samples: list[dict], max_len: int, ttt_steps: int,
320	                      device: str | torch.device, dtype: torch.dtype,
321	                      min_seg_len: int = 64,
322	                      pin_memory: bool = False) -> dict:
323	    """Build a (B=1) packed batch.
324	
325	    Tail masking is applied to ``assistant_mask`` on whichever device we
326	    end on. ``pin_memory=True`` is honored only when device==cpu (CUDA
327	    tensors are already in device memory).
328	
329	    Stream-separation pattern: caller passes ``device='cpu', pin_memory=True``
330	    inside a worker thread, then in the main thread does
331	    ``{k: v.to('cuda', non_blocking=True) for k, v in batch.items()}`` so
332	    H2D copy runs on PyTorch's default copy stream and overlaps with
333	    forward compute on the main stream.
334	    """
335	    packed = pack_samples(samples, max_len, min_seg_len=min_seg_len)
336	    if packed is None:
337	        raise RuntimeError("make_packed_batch: pack_samples returned None")
338	
339	    n_real = packed["token_ids"].shape[0]
340	    if n_real < max_len:
341	        pad_len = max_len - n_real
342	        for k, t in list(packed.items()):
343	            if k.startswith("_") or not torch.is_tensor(t):
344	                continue
345	            pad_shape = list(t.shape)
346	            pad_shape[0] = pad_len
347	            pad_value = 0
348	            pad = torch.full(pad_shape, pad_value, dtype=t.dtype)
349	            packed[k] = torch.cat([t, pad], dim=0)
350	        pad_doc = int(packed["document_ids"][n_real - 1].item()) + 1
351	        packed["document_ids"][n_real:] = pad_doc
352	        packed["assistant_mask"][n_real:] = False
353	
354	    # Build batch on CPU first (cheap dtype casts only), then move/pin.
355	    out = {
356	        "token_ids": packed["token_ids"].unsqueeze(0).long(),
357	        "aux_hidden": packed["aux_hidden"].unsqueeze(0).to(dtype),
358	        "target_logits_values": packed["top_logit_values"].unsqueeze(0).to(torch.float32),
359	        "target_logits_indices": packed["top_logit_indices"].unsqueeze(0).long(),
360	        "target_logsumexp": packed["target_logsumexp"].unsqueeze(0).to(torch.float32),
361	        "assistant_mask": packed["assistant_mask"].unsqueeze(0).bool(),
362	        "document_ids": packed["document_ids"].unsqueeze(0).long(),
363	        "is_packed": True,
364	    }
365	    # Apply TTT tail masking on CPU before the H2D copy so the GPU never
366	    # sees an unmasked tail (and so we can keep the .to() unconditional).
367	    out["assistant_mask"] = mask_segment_tail(
368	        out["assistant_mask"], out["document_ids"], n_pad=ttt_steps
369	    )
370	
371	    is_cpu_target = (str(device) == "cpu")
372	    if pin_memory and is_cpu_target and torch.cuda.is_available():
373	        for k, v in list(out.items()):
374	            if torch.is_tensor(v):
375	                out[k] = v.pin_memory()
376	    elif not is_cpu_target:
377	        for k, v in list(out.items()):
378	            if torch.is_tensor(v):
379	                out[k] = v.to(device, non_blocking=True)
380	    return out
381
```

```
1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	# Train SALA EAGLE-3 draft on the merged v2mix_20k dataset (10k base +
3	# 10k incremental, hard-linked under v2mix_20k/).
4	#
5	# This run uses every optimisation we landed in the recent refactor:
6	#   * NVFP4 forward GEMM on sm_120 (~+14% throughput vs bf16 STE)
7	#   * sequence packing (EAGLE_DRAFT_PACK=1) — eliminates padding waste on
8	#     variable-length samples; lm_head kept in bf16 by default.
9	#   * BS=4 × GRAD_ACCUM=4 = effective batch 16 (current max stable, see
10	#     bench_max_bs.py)
11	#
12	# Run directly (not in a backgrounded shell), e.g.:
13	#   bash eagle/bin/train_v2mix_20k.sh
14	#   bash eagle/bin/train_v2mix_20k.sh 2>&1 | tee /tmp/train_v2mix_20k.log
15	set -euo pipefail
16	cd /user_4813494d/openbmb
17	
18	DATA_DIR="eagle/data/target_regen/v2mix_20k"
19	OUTPUT_DIR="eagle/weights/v2mix_20k"
20	STEPS="${STEPS:-5000}"
21	EVAL_EVERY="${EVAL_EVERY:-250}"
22	
23	# Optional: validation dirs are skipped gracefully if absent (no val_ind/ood
24	# data exists yet for the merged set). Override via env if you build them.
25	VAL_IND_DIR="${VAL_IND_DIR:-eagle/data/target_regen_val/ind_200}"
26	VAL_OOD_DIR="${VAL_OOD_DIR:-eagle/data/target_regen_val/ood_bench64}"
27	
28	if [[ ! -d "${DATA_DIR}" ]]; then
29	    echo "missing data: ${DATA_DIR}" >&2
30	    exit 1
31	fi
32	mkdir -p "${OUTPUT_DIR}"
33	
34	echo "============================================================"
35	echo "  SALA draft train v2mix_20k"
36	echo "============================================================"
37	echo "  data       : ${DATA_DIR}  ($(ls "${DATA_DIR}"/*.pt 2>/dev/null | wc -l) files)"
38	echo "  output     : ${OUTPUT_DIR}"
39	echo "  steps      : ${STEPS}"
40	echo "  eval_every : ${EVAL_EVERY}"
41	echo "  val_ind    : ${VAL_IND_DIR} ($([[ -d ${VAL_IND_DIR} ]] && echo present || echo absent))"
42	echo "  val_ood    : ${VAL_OOD_DIR} ($([[ -d ${VAL_OOD_DIR} ]] && echo present || echo absent))"
43	echo "  effective batch : 4 × 4 = 16"
44	echo "  packing    : ON  (EAGLE_DRAFT_PACK=1)"
45	echo "  NVFP4 fwd  : ON  (EAGLE_NVFP4_FORWARD=1, lm_head excluded)"
46	echo "============================================================"
47	
48	export PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF="expandable_segments:True"
49	
50	# Architecture / training hyper-params (v4 sala_draft defaults; pinned here
51	# so a future env-default change does not silently shift this run).
52	export EAGLE_DRAFT_BATCH_SIZE=4
53	export EAGLE_DRAFT_GRAD_ACCUM=4
54	export EAGLE_DRAFT_SEQ_LEN_MAX=4096
55	export EAGLE_DRAFT_TTT_STEPS=3
56	
57	# Sequence packing — multiple short samples per microbatch, cross-doc isolation
58	# enforced by document_ids + per-step bool mask.
59	export EAGLE_DRAFT_PACK=1
60	export EAGLE_DRAFT_PACK_MIN_SEG=64
61	
62	# NVFP4 forward GEMM (sm_120, flashinfer cutlass backend). lm_head stays bf16.
63	export EAGLE_NVFP4_FORWARD=1
64	export EAGLE_NVFP4_EXCLUDE_LM_HEAD=1
65	
66	# Inference-side autotune cache (loaded lazily on first FP4 GEMM).
67	export SGLANG_FP4_TUNE_CACHE="/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/assets/mm_fp4_tune_sm120.json"
68	
69	python3 eagle/training/sala_draft/train.py \
70	    --steps "${STEPS}" \
71	    --eval_every "${EVAL_EVERY}" \
72	    --data_dir "${DATA_DIR}" \
73	    --val_ind_dir "${VAL_IND_DIR}" \
```

```
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Profile current best train step to identify the hottest GPU ops.
3	
4	Single-mode: best path = async builder + cpu pinned + main H2D + fused AdamW.
5	Use torch.profiler to capture 3 bench steps after a 2-step warmup; print
6	top ops by self CUDA time.
7	
8	Run: python3 eagle/training/sala_draft/profile_hot_ops.py
9	"""
10	from __future__ import annotations
11	
12	import os
13	import random
14	import sys
15	import time
16	from pathlib import Path
17	
18	REPO_user_4813494d = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
19	sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO_user_4813494d))
20	
21	os.environ.setdefault("PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF", "expandable_segments:True")
22	os.environ.setdefault("EAGLE_DRAFT_TTT_STEPS", "3")
23	os.environ.setdefault("EAGLE_DRAFT_SEQ_LEN_MAX", "4096")
24	os.environ.setdefault("EAGLE_DRAFT_PACK", "1")
25	
26	import torch
27	import torch.profiler as tp
28	
29	from eagle.legacy.v2_v3 import train as v3train
30	from eagle.training.sala_draft import train as v4train
31	from eagle.training.sala_draft.packing import (
32	    PackedFileSampler, index_lengths, make_packed_batch,
33	)
34	
35	
36	def build_model_and_opt():
37	    embed_w, lm_head_w = v3train.load_embed_and_lm_head()
38	    model = v4train.Eagle3ModelV4(embed_w, lm_head_w).to(v4train.DEVICE).to(v4train.DTYPE)
39	    model.load_v4_vocab_cache(Path(v4train.VOCAB_CACHE_V4))
40	    v3train.init_mlp_from_target(model)
41	    model.train()
42	    decay, no_decay = [], []
43	    for name, p in model.named_parameters():
44	        if not p.requires_grad: continue
45	        (no_decay if (name.endswith(".bias") or name.endswith("norm.weight")) else decay).append(p)
46	    opt = torch.optim.AdamW(
47	        [{"params": decay, "weight_decay": 0.01},
48	         {"params": no_decay, "weight_decay": 0.0}],
49	        lr=5e-4, betas=(0.9, 0.95), fused=True,
50	    )
51	    return model, opt
52	
53	
54	def make_build(sampler, BS, S, T):
55	    def build_one():
56	        bin_dicts = []
57	        for _ in range(BS):
58	            bf = sampler.next_bin()
59	            ss = [v4train.load_sample_v4(f) for f in bf]
60	            bin_dicts.append(make_packed_batch(
61	                ss, max_len=S, ttt_steps=T, device="cpu",
62	                dtype=v4train.DTYPE, min_seg_len=64, pin_memory=False,
63	            ))
64	        out = {}
65	        for k in bin_dicts[0]:
66	            v0 = bin_dicts[0][k]
67	            if torch.is_tensor(v0):
68	                t = torch.cat([d[k] for d in bin_dicts], dim=0)
69	                if torch.cuda.is_available():
70	                    t = t.pin_memory()
71	                out[k] = t
72	            else:
73	                out[k] = v0
74	        return out
75	    return build_one
76	
77	
78	def to_cuda(batch):
79	    return {
80	        k: (v.to(v4train.DEVICE, non_blocking=True) if torch.is_tensor(v) else v)
81	        for k, v in batch.items()
82	    }
83	
84	
85	def main():
86	    print(f"PyTorch {torch.__version__} on {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}")
87	    BS, S, T, GA = 4, 4096, 3, 4
88	    print(f"config: BS={BS} GA={GA} S={S} T={T}  effective={BS*GA}\n")
89	
90	    model, opt = build_model_and_opt()
91	
92	    data_dir = Path("eagle/data/target_regen/v2mix_20k")
93	    files = sorted(data_dir.glob("*.pt"))
94	    lengths = index_lengths(data_dir, data_dir.parent / f"{data_dir.name}.lengths.json", verbose=False)
95	    sampler = PackedFileSampler(files=files, lengths=lengths, max_len=S, min_seg_len=64,
96	                                ttt_pad=T, rng=random.Random(0))
97	    build_one = make_build(sampler, BS, S, T)
98	    pf = v4train._BatchPrefetcher(build_one, queue_size=2,
99	                                  on_error=lambda e: print(f"prefetch err: {e}"))
100	
101	    try:
102	        # Warmup (no profile)
103	        for _ in range(2):
104	            opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
105	            for _ in range(GA):
106	                batch = pf.next_batch()
107	                if batch is None: continue
108	                batch = to_cuda(batch)
109	                loss, _, _ = model(**batch)
110	                (loss / GA).backward()
111	            opt.step()
112	        torch.cuda.synchronize()
113	
114	        # Profile 3 steps
115	        print("[profile] capturing 3 step traces …")
116	        with tp.profile(
117	            activities=[tp.ProfilerActivity.CPU, tp.ProfilerActivity.CUDA],
118	            record_shapes=False, with_stack=False,
119	        ) as prof:
120	            for _ in range(3):
121	                opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
122	                for _ in range(GA):
123	                    batch = pf.next_batch()
124	                    if batch is None: continue
125	                    batch = to_cuda(batch)
126	                    loss, _, _ = model(**batch)
127	                    (loss / GA).backward()
128	                opt.step()
129	            torch.cuda.synchronize()
130	    finally:
131	        pf.close()
132	
133	    print("\n=== top 20 ops by self CUDA time ===")
134	    print(prof.key_averages().table(sort_by="cuda_time_total", row_limit=20))
135	
136	
137	if __name__ == "__main__":
138	    main()
139
```

```json
}
```

Excellent. Now let me get a summary of data sizes for the full dataset and check how data loading works at inference time:

> DEVELOPER

深入研究 /user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/ 目录下的 EAGLE-3 draft 训练工作区，目标是为后续在单卡 RTX PRO 6000D (84 GB VRAM, sm_120) 上完成 300K 样本训练做技术准备。**注意我们的数据已经在 BOS 云端：`bos://anp3-common-model/vista/eagle3_data/v3mix_300k_det_len2048_topk256_aux1-10-22`，共 4688 个 segments，每个 segment 64 个 .pt 文件，每文件平均 ~12 MiB，本地已无数据**。

请用 "very thorough" 模式调查并写一份不超过 1000 字的报告，覆盖以下问题：

## 1. NVFP4 压缩管线（关键）
- 阅读 `eagle/nvfp4_codec.py`，说明 aux_hidden 是怎么从 bf16 (12288 dim) 压成 packed FP4 + bf16 scale 的：压缩比、bit-exactness 是否成立、磁盘 .pt 里实际字段（packed shape, scale shape, 其他元数据）。
- 阅读 `eagle/pipelines/target_regen/v3mix/collect_nvfp4_bos.py` 和 `eagle/bin/collect_v3mix_nvfp4_bos.sh`，搞清 server 端的 hook（`EAGLE3_ONESTAGE_NVFP4=1`）是直接写 NVFP4 还是 client 再压缩。每个 .pt 文件里实际存了哪些 tensor（token_ids / aux_packed / aux_scale / top_logit_values / top_logit_indices / target_logsumexp / assistant_mask / document_ids）和它们的 dtype/shape。给出一个文件解码后的内存占用估算（seq_len=2048, K=256）。

## 2. 训练时 NVFP4 forward
- 阅读 `eagle/core/fp4_quant.py`，分清三件事：
  - 训练时权重的 FP4 forward（`_NVFP4LinearFn` + `fp4_quant_freeze`），用 `sgl_kernel.scaled_fp4_quant` + `flashinfer.mm_fp4`，backward 还是 bf16 STE。
  - 这跟 `nvfp4_codec.py` 里的 aux_hidden 压缩**完全是两件事**——不要混淆。
  - TTT_STEPS=3 时 freeze/unfreeze 怎么节约重复量化。
- 阅读 `eagle/training/sala_draft/train.py` 的 `load_sample_v4` 和 `make_batch_v4` / `make_packed_batch`，搞清训练时数据是怎么从 .pt 流转到 GPU 的：

  - prefetcher 几个 worker、pinned memory 何时生效。
  - 单个 microbatch 实际 GPU 显存占用估算（BS=4, GRAD_ACCUM=4, SEQ_LEN=4096, TTT_STEPS=3, K=256）。

```
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""NVFP4 aux_hidden storage codec for EAGLE target-regenerated data.
3	
4	The collector receives bf16 ``aux_hidden`` from the target hook.  For large
5	target-regenerated runs this tensor dominates disk usage, so final training
6	files store it as packed FP4 E2M1 values plus bf16 per-16-channel scales.  The
7	trainer decodes it back to bf16 on load and keeps the model forward unchanged.
8	"""
9	from __future__ import annotations
10	
11	from typing import Any
12	
13	import torch
14	
15	
16	FP4_GROUP_SIZE = 16
17	FP4_MAX = 6.0
18	FORMAT_TAG = "nvfp4_aux_v1"
19	LEGACY_FORMAT_TAG = "nvfp4_v1"
20	
21	_FP4_POS = torch.tensor(
22	    [0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0, 6.0], dtype=torch.float32
23	)
24	_FP4_BOUNDS = torch.tensor(
25	    [0.25, 0.75, 1.25, 1.75, 2.5, 3.5, 5.0], dtype=torch.float32
26	)
27	
28	
29	def is_nvfp4_sample(d: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
30	    return (
31	        d.get("format") in {FORMAT_TAG, LEGACY_FORMAT_TAG}
32	        or ("aux_packed" in d and "aux_scale" in d and "aux_hidden" not in d)
33	    )
34	
35	
36	def encode(x: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
37	    """Encode ``(..., D)`` bf16-like tensor into packed NVFP4.
38	
39	    Returns:
40	        packed: ``(..., D // 2)`` uint8, two FP4 codes per byte.
41	        scale: ``(..., D // 16)`` bf16, per-group scale.
42	    """
43	    if x.shape[-1] % FP4_GROUP_SIZE != 0:
44	        raise ValueError(
45	            f"last dim {x.shape[-1]} not divisible by {FP4_GROUP_SIZE}"
46	        )
47	    if x.shape[-1] % 2 != 0:
48	        raise ValueError("last dim must be even for byte packing")
49	    if x.dtype != torch.bfloat16:
50	        x = x.to(torch.bfloat16)
51	
52	    *prefix, dim = x.shape
53	    n_groups = dim // FP4_GROUP_SIZE
54	    grouped = x.contiguous().reshape(*prefix, n_groups, FP4_GROUP_SIZE)
55	    scale = (
56	        grouped.abs().amax(-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-8) / FP4_MAX
57	    ).to(torch.bfloat16)
58	    q = grouped / scale
59	
60	    bounds = _FP4_BOUNDS.to(device=q.device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)
61	    abs_idx = torch.bucketize(
62	        q.abs().clamp(max=FP4_MAX), bounds, out_int32=True
63	    ).to(torch.uint8)
64	    # Canonicalize FP4 zero: code 8 decodes to the same numeric zero as code 0,
65	    # but avoiding negative-zero codes makes packed validation deterministic.
66	    sign_bit = ((q < 0) & (abs_idx != 0)).to(torch.uint8).mul_(8)
67	    codes = (abs_idx | sign_bit).reshape(*prefix, dim)
68	    pair = codes.reshape(*prefix, dim // 2, 2)
69	    packed = (pair[..., 0] | (pair[..., 1] << 4)).to(torch.uint8)
70	    return packed.contiguous(), scale.squeeze(-1).contiguous()
71	
72	
73	def decode(
74	    packed: torch.Tensor,
75	    scale: torch.Tensor,
76	    out_dtype: torch.dtype = torch.bfloat16,
77	) -> torch.Tensor:
78	    """Decode packed NVFP4 tensors back to dense bf16 by default."""
79	    if packed.dtype != torch.uint8:
80	        raise TypeError(f"packed must be uint8, got {packed.dtype}")
81	    dim = packed.shape[-1] * 2
82	    low = (packed & 0x0F).to(torch.long)
83	    high = ((packed >> 4) & 0x0F).to(torch.long)
84	    codes = torch.stack([low, high], dim=-1).reshape(*packed.shape[:-1], dim)
85	
86	    pos = _FP4_POS.to(device=codes.device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)
87	    sign = torch.where(
88	        (codes & 8) != 0,
89	        torch.tensor(-1.0, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=codes.device),
90	        torch.tensor(1.0, dtype=torch.bfloat16, device=codes.device),
91	    )
92	    fp4_vals = pos[codes & 7] * sign
93	
94	    n_groups = dim // FP4_GROUP_SIZE
95	    fp4_vals = fp4_vals.reshape(*fp4_vals.shape[:-1], n_groups, FP4_GROUP_SIZE)
96	    out = (fp4_vals * scale.unsqueeze(-1)).reshape(*packed.shape[:-1], dim)
97	    return out.to(out_dtype)
98	
99	
100	def compress_sample(d: dict[str, Any], *, keep_aux_hidden: bool = False) -> dict[str, Any]:
101	    """Return a copy of ``d`` with ``aux_hidden`` stored as NVFP4 payload.
102	
103	    Unlike the legacy v2/v3 codec, this preserves all current target_regen
104	    metadata fields such as ``assistant_mask``, ``document_ids`` and
105	    ``target_logsumexp``.
106	    """
107	    if "aux_hidden" not in d:
108	        if is_nvfp4_sample(d):
109	            return d
110	        raise KeyError("sample has neither aux_hidden nor aux_packed/aux_scale")
111	
112	    packed, scale = encode(d["aux_hidden"])
113	    out = dict(d)
114	    if not keep_aux_hidden:
115	        out.pop("aux_hidden", None)
116	    out["aux_packed"] = packed
117	    out["aux_scale"] = scale
118	    out["aux_hidden_shape"] = tuple(d["aux_hidden"].shape)
119	    out["format"] = FORMAT_TAG
120	
121	    if "token_ids" in out and torch.is_tensor(out["token_ids"]):
122	        out["token_ids"] = out["token_ids"].to(torch.int64).contiguous()
123	    if "top_logit_values" in out and torch.is_tensor(out["top_logit_values"]):
124	        out["top_logit_values"] = out["top_logit_values"].to(torch.bfloat16).contiguous()
125	    if "top_logit_indices" in out and torch.is_tensor(out["top_logit_indices"]):
126	        out["top_logit_indices"] = out["top_logit_indices"].to(torch.int32).contiguous()
127	    return out
128	
129	
130	def decompress_sample(
131	    d: dict[str, Any],
132	    *,
133	    keep_packed: bool = False,
134	    out_dtype: torch.dtype = torch.bfloat16,
135	) -> dict[str, Any]:
136	    """Return a copy of an NVFP4 sample with dense ``aux_hidden`` restored."""
137	    if not is_nvfp4_sample(d):
138	        return d
139	    out = dict(d)
140	    out["aux_hidden"] = decode(out["aux_packed"], out["aux_scale"], out_dtype=out_dtype)
141	    if not keep_packed:
142	        out.pop("aux_packed", None)
143	        out.pop("aux_scale", None)
144	    return out
145	
146	
147	def _in_memory_roundtrip(x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
148	    *prefix, dim = x.shape
149	    grouped = x.reshape(*prefix, dim // FP4_GROUP_SIZE, FP4_GROUP_SIZE)
150	    scale = grouped.abs().amax(-1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1e-8) / FP4_MAX
151	    scale_bf16 = scale.to(torch.bfloat16).to(scale.dtype)
152	    bounds = _FP4_BOUNDS.to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
153	    pos = _FP4_POS.to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
154	    q = grouped / scale_bf16
155	    sign = q.sign()
156	    idx = torch.bucketize(q.abs().clamp(max=FP4_MAX), bounds)
157	    return (sign * pos[idx] * scale_bf16).to(x.dtype).reshape(*prefix, dim)
158	
159	
160	def _test_roundtrip() -> None:
161	    torch.manual_seed(0)
162	    shapes = [(2048, 12288), (128, 4096), (7, 3, 64), (32, 16)]
163	    for shape in shapes:
164	        x = torch.randn(*shape, dtype=torch.bfloat16) * 3.0
165	        packed, scale = encode(x)
166	        decoded = decode(packed, scale)
167	        ref = _in_memory_roundtrip(x)
168	        equal = torch.equal(decoded, ref)
169	        print(
170	            f"shape={shape} packed={tuple(packed.shape)} scale={tuple(scale.shape)} "
171	            f"bit_exact={equal}"
172	        )
173	        if not equal:
174	            raise AssertionError(f"roundtrip mismatch for {shape}")
175	
176	
177	def _test_disk(tmp_path: str = "/tmp/eagle_nvfp4_codec_test.pt") -> None:
178	    import os
179	
180	    raw = {
181	        "token_ids": torch.randint(0, 73000, (128,), dtype=torch.int64),
182	        "aux_hidden": torch.randn(128, 12288, dtype=torch.bfloat16),
183	        "top_logit_values": torch.randn(128, 128, dtype=torch.bfloat16),
184	        "top_logit_indices": torch.randint(0, 73000, (128, 128), dtype=torch.int32),
185	        "target_logsumexp": torch.randn(128, dtype=torch.float32),
186	        "assistant_mask": torch.ones(128, dtype=torch.bool),
187	        "document_ids": torch.zeros(128, dtype=torch.int32),
188	    }
189	    compressed = compress_sample(raw)
190	    torch.save(compressed, tmp_path)
191	    loaded = torch.load(tmp_path, weights_only=True, map_location="cpu")
192	    decoded = decompress_sample(loaded)
193	    ref = _in_memory_roundtrip(raw["aux_hidden"])
194	    if not torch.equal(decoded["aux_hidden"], ref):
195	        raise AssertionError("disk roundtrip mismatch")
196	    print(f"disk_roundtrip_ok size_mb={os.path.getsize(tmp_path) / 1024 / 1024:.2f}")
197	    os.unlink(tmp_path)
198	
199	
200	if __name__ == "__main__":
201	    _test_roundtrip()
202	    _test_disk()
203	    print("All NVFP4 codec tests passed")
204
```

```
1	"""FP4 quantization for EAGLE-3 draft training.
2	
3	Two forward modes share one ``FP4QATLinear`` interface:
4	
5	  * **NVFP4_FORWARD=1** (default when sgl-kernel + flashinfer present, sm_120):
6	    Real 4-bit GEMM via ``flashinfer.mm_fp4(use_nvfp4=True, backend="cutlass")``.
7	    Backward is bf16 STE — gradients flow through the master bf16 weight as if
8	    fake-quant, so optimizer behaviour is unchanged.
9	
10	  * **NVFP4_FORWARD=0**: Legacy fake-STE — bf16 GEMM with FP4-rounded weight
11	    (``_FP4QuantSTE``). Identical math, ~5x slower.
12	
13	Public API:
14	    FP4QATLinear           drop-in replacement for nn.Linear
15	    fp4_quant_freeze(model)   pre-quantize weights once per training step so
16	                              multi-step (TTT) forwards reuse the cached FP4
17	                              representation
18	    fp4_quant_unfreeze(model) clear cache (called at end of forward)
19	
20	Toggles (env):
21	    EAGLE_NVFP4_FORWARD=0/1   (default 1)  switch between true 4-bit and fake STE
22	    EAGLE_NVFP4_EXCLUDE_LM_HEAD=0/1 (default 1)  skip lm_head; vocab-projection
23	                              quant-noise leaks into every token's softmax
24	    SGLANG_FP4_TUNE_CACHE     path to flashinfer autotune JSON (loaded lazily)
25	
26	Convention (per ``sgl-kernel/tests/test_fp4_gemm.py``):
27	    gs = (E4M3_max * FP4_max) / amax = 2688 / amax    (multiplied onto input)
28	    alpha = 1 / (gs_a * gs_b)                          (descales output)
29	"""
30	from __future__ import annotations
31	
32	import os
33	
34	import torch
35	import torch.nn as nn
36	import torch.nn.functional as F
37	
38	
39	# ── Switches & constants ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
40	
41	FP4_QAT = True
42	FP4_GROUP_SIZE = 16  # NVFP4 group size (16 weights share one FP8 scale)
43	
44	# E4M3_max * FP4_max — joint upper bound of NVFP4's representable range.
45	_FP4_E4M3_E2M1_PRODUCT = 448.0 * 6.0   # = 2688
46	
47	# FP4 E2M1 positive values and their rounding boundaries (for STE round path)
48	_FP4_POS = torch.tensor([0.0, 0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0, 6.0])
49	_FP4_BOUNDS = torch.tensor([0.25, 0.75, 1.25, 1.75, 2.5, 3.5, 5.0])
50	
51	
52	# ── Optional NVFP4 kernels (sgl-kernel + flashinfer) ──────────────────────
53	
54	try:
55	    from sgl_kernel import scaled_fp4_quant as _sgl_scaled_fp4_quant
56	    from flashinfer import mm_fp4 as _fi_mm_fp4
57	    _NVFP4_FORWARD_AVAILABLE = True
58	except Exception:
59	    _sgl_scaled_fp4_quant = None
60	    _fi_mm_fp4 = None
61	    _NVFP4_FORWARD_AVAILABLE = False
62	
63	NVFP4_FORWARD = (
64	    int(os.environ.get("EAGLE_NVFP4_FORWARD", "1")) and _NVFP4_FORWARD_AVAILABLE
65	)
66	NVFP4_EXCLUDE_LM_HEAD = int(os.environ.get("EAGLE_NVFP4_EXCLUDE_LM_HEAD", "1"))
67	
68	_NVFP4_AUTOTUNE_LOADED = False
69	
70	
71	def _maybe_load_nvfp4_autotune() -> None:
72	    """Load the inference-side flashinfer autotune cache once (idempotent).
73	
74	    Bench data shows this cache yields no measurable gain on training-size M
75	    (4095 / 16380) — but loading is harmless and shares the inference table.
76	    """
77	    global _NVFP4_AUTOTUNE_LOADED
78	    if _NVFP4_AUTOTUNE_LOADED or not _NVFP4_FORWARD_AVAILABLE:
79	        return
80	    cache_path = os.environ.get(
81	        "SGLANG_FP4_TUNE_CACHE",
82	        "/user_4813494d/openbmb/demo-sala/assets/mm_fp4_tune_sm120.json",
83	    )
84	    if not os.path.exists(cache_path):
85	        _NVFP4_AUTOTUNE_LOADED = True
86	        return
87	    try:
88	        from flashinfer.autotuner import AutoTuner
89	        AutoTuner.get().load_configs(cache_path)
90	    except Exception:

92	    _NVFP4_AUTOTUNE_LOADED = True
93	
94	
95	# ── STE round (legacy fake-quant path) ────────────────────────────────────
96	
97	def _fp4_round(x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
98	    """Round each element to the nearest FP4 E2M1 representable value.
99	
100	    Input: any float tensor (preferably already in [-6, 6]).
101	    Output: same dtype, values in {0, ±0.5, ±1, ±1.5, ±2, ±3, ±4, ±6}.
102	    """
103	    pos = _FP4_POS.to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
104	    bounds = _FP4_BOUNDS.to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
105	    sign = x.sign()
106	    idx = torch.bucketize(x.abs().clamp(max=6.0), bounds)
107	    return sign * pos[idx]
108	
109	
110	class _FP4QuantSTE(torch.autograd.Function):
111	    """Fake-quantize weight to FP4 E2M1 in forward; straight-through in backward.
112	
113	    Per-group-of-16 scaling: ``scale = max(|w_group|) / 6.0``. Stays in the
114	    weight's native dtype (bf16); only the per-group scale (1 value per 16
115	    weights) goes through fp32 for clamp stability.
116	    """
117	    @staticmethod
118	    def forward(ctx, weight: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
119	        N, K = weight.shape
120	        w_g = weight.reshape(N, K // FP4_GROUP_SIZE, FP4_GROUP_SIZE)
121	        abs_max = w_g.abs().amax(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
122	        scale = (abs_max.float().clamp(min=1e-8) / 6.0).to(weight.dtype)
123	        w_q = _fp4_round(w_g / scale) * scale
124	        return w_q.reshape(N, K)
125	
126	    @staticmethod
127	    def backward(ctx, grad: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
128	        return grad  # STE
129	
130	
131	# ── True 4-bit forward path (NVFP4 cutlass) ───────────────────────────────
132	
133	def _compute_global_scale(t: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
134	    """Per-tensor input_global_scale matching the NVFP4 reference."""
135	    amax = t.detach().abs().amax().to(torch.float32).clamp(min=1e-6)
136	    return (_FP4_E4M3_E2M1_PRODUCT / amax).view(1)
137	
138	
139	class _NVFP4LinearFn(torch.autograd.Function):
140	    """Forward: NVFP4 GEMM (true 4-bit). Backward: bf16 STE.
141	
142	    NVFP4 official scale convention applied so input_global_scale * input fits
143	    the joint FP4*E4M3 dynamic range. ``alpha = 1 / (gs_x * gs_w)`` descales
144	    the output back to the original magnitude. Without this, weights with
145	    small amax (e.g. ~0.01 after init_mlp_from_target) trigger e4m3 underflow
146	    and the GEMM produces NaN on entire blocks.
147	
148	    STE backward: forward uses the FP4-quantized weight, but the gradient
149	    w.r.t. the master bf16 weight is the standard linear gradient
150	    (grad_w = grad_out.T @ x), identical to the legacy fake-quant STE.
151	    """
152	    @staticmethod
153	    def forward(ctx, x, weight_bf16, wq, wsf, w_gs):
154	        _maybe_load_nvfp4_autotune()
155	        x2d = x.reshape(-1, x.shape[-1]).contiguous()
156	        x_gs = _compute_global_scale(x2d)
157	        xq, xsf = _sgl_scaled_fp4_quant(x2d, x_gs)
158	        alpha = (1.0 / (x_gs * w_gs)).contiguous()
159	        out2d = _fi_mm_fp4(
160	            xq, wq.T, xsf, wsf.T, alpha,
161	            out_dtype=torch.bfloat16,
162	            block_size=16, use_nvfp4=True, backend="cutlass",
163	        )
164	        out_shape = list(x.shape[:-1]) + [out2d.shape[-1]]
165	        ctx.save_for_backward(x, weight_bf16)
166	        return out2d.view(out_shape)
167	
168	    @staticmethod
169	    def backward(ctx, grad_out):
170	        x, w = ctx.saved_tensors
171	        grad_out_2d = grad_out.reshape(-1, grad_out.shape[-1])
172	        x_2d = x.reshape(-1, x.shape[-1])
173	        grad_x = (grad_out_2d @ w).view_as(x)
174	        grad_w = grad_out_2d.t() @ x_2d
175	        return grad_x, grad_w, None, None, None
176	
177	
178	# ── Linear wrapper ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
179	
180	class FP4QATLinear(nn.Linear):
181	    """nn.Linear with FP4 fake-quantization on weights during training.
182	
183	    Eval / FP4_QAT=False / ``_fp4_skip=True``: plain bf16 ``F.linear`` so the
184	    final RTN export matches inference exactly.
185	    """
186	    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
187	        if not (self.training and FP4_QAT):
188	            return F.linear(x, self.weight, self.bias)
189	        if getattr(self, "_fp4_skip", False):
190	            return F.linear(x, self.weight, self.bias)
191	
192	        if NVFP4_FORWARD:
193	            wq = getattr(self, "_fp4_qw_packed", None)
194	            wsf = getattr(self, "_fp4_qw_sf", None)
195	            w_gs = getattr(self, "_fp4_w_gs", None)
196	            if wq is None:
197	                w_gs = _compute_global_scale(self.weight)
198	                wq, wsf = _sgl_scaled_fp4_quant(self.weight, w_gs)
199	            out = _NVFP4LinearFn.apply(x, self.weight, wq, wsf, w_gs)
200	            return out if self.bias is None else (out + self.bias)
201	
202	        # Legacy fake-STE (bf16 GEMM, FP4-rounded weight).
203	        qw = getattr(self, "_fp4_qw", None)
204	        if qw is None:
205	            qw = _FP4QuantSTE.apply(self.weight)
206	        return F.linear(x, qw, self.bias)
207	
208	
209	def fp4_quant_freeze(model: nn.Module) -> None:
210	    """Pre-quantize every FP4QATLinear weight so multi-step forwards reuse it.
211	
212	    NVFP4_FORWARD=1: caches packed (wq, wsf, w_gs) — weight quantize cost is
213	    paid once per optimizer step instead of TTT_STEPS times.
214	    NVFP4_FORWARD=0: caches fake-STE quantized weight (bf16 tensor).
215	    """
216	    if not FP4_QAT:
217	        return
218	    for m in model.modules():
219	        if isinstance(m, FP4QATLinear) and m.training:
220	            if getattr(m, "_fp4_skip", False):
221	                continue
222	            if NVFP4_FORWARD:
223	                with torch.no_grad():
224	                    w_gs = _compute_global_scale(m.weight)
225	                    wq, wsf = _sgl_scaled_fp4_quant(m.weight, w_gs)
226	                m._fp4_qw_packed = wq
227	                m._fp4_qw_sf = wsf
228	                m._fp4_w_gs = w_gs
229	            else:
230	                m._fp4_qw = _FP4QuantSTE.apply(m.weight)
231	
232	
233	def fp4_quant_unfreeze(model: nn.Module) -> None:
234	    """Clear the per-step quantized-weight cache after backward."""
235	    for m in model.modules():
236	        if isinstance(m, FP4QATLinear):
237	            m._fp4_qw = None
238	            m._fp4_qw_packed = None
239	            m._fp4_qw_sf = None
240	            m._fp4_w_gs = None
241
```

```
38	def load_jsonl(path: Path, start: int, limit: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:

64	def disk_free_gb(path: Path) -> float:
65	    target = path
66	    while not target.exists() and target != target.parent:
67	        target = target.parent

93	def append_jsonl(path: Path, record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
94	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

99	def sha256_file(path: Path, block_size: int = 8 << 20) -> str:
100	    h = hashlib.sha256()
101	    with path.open("rb") as f:
102	        while True:
103	            chunk = f.read(block_size)
104	            if not chunk:
105	                break
106	            h.update(chunk)
107	    return h.hexdigest()

128	class State:
129	    def __init__(self, path: Path):
130	        self.path = path
131	        self.next_idx = 0
132	        self.next_segment_idx = 0
133	        self.uploaded_segments: set[int] = set()
134	        self.n_uploaded_files = 0
135	        self.n_uploaded_bytes = 0
136	        self._lock = threading.Lock()
137	        self.load()
138	
139	    def load(self) -> None:
140	        if not self.path.exists():
141	            return
142	        try:
143	            d = json.loads(self.path.read_text())
144	            self.next_idx = int(d.get("next_idx", 0))
145	            self.next_segment_idx = int(d.get("next_segment_idx", 0))
146	            self.uploaded_segments = set(map(int, d.get("uploaded_segments", [])))
147	            self.n_uploaded_files = int(d.get("n_uploaded_files", 0))
148	            self.n_uploaded_bytes = int(d.get("n_uploaded_bytes", 0))
149	        except Exception as e:
150	            print(f"[state] ignoring unreadable state {self.path}: {e}")
151	
152	    def save(self) -> None:
153	        with self._lock:
154	            self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
155	            tmp = self.path.with_suffix(self.path.suffix + ".tmp")
156	            tmp.write_text(json.dumps({
157	                "next_idx": self.next_idx,
158	                "next_segment_idx": self.next_segment_idx,
159	                "uploaded_segments": sorted(self.uploaded_segments),
160	                "n_uploaded_files": self.n_uploaded_files,
161	                "n_uploaded_bytes": self.n_uploaded_bytes,
162	                "updated_at": time.time(),
163	            }, indent=1))
164	            tmp.replace(self.path)
165	
166	    def mark_uploaded(self, seg_idx: int, n_files: int, n_bytes: int) -> None:
167	        with self._lock:
168	            self.uploaded_segments.add(seg_idx)
169	            self.n_uploaded_files += n_files
170	            self.n_uploaded_bytes += n_bytes
171	        self.save()

173	
174	class BosUploader:
175	    def __init__(self, args, state: State, pbar: tqdm | None):
176	        self.args = args
177	        self.state = state
178	        self.pbar = pbar
179	        self.queue: Queue = Queue()
180	        self.stop = threading.Event()
181	        self.client = None
182	        self.bucket = ""
183	        self.prefix = ""
184	        self.method = args.upload_method
185	        self.uploaded_bytes_this_run = 0
186	        self.uploaded_files_this_run = 0
187	        self.upload_seconds_this_run = 0.0
188	        self._stats_lock = threading.Lock()
189	        self._fatal_error: str | None = None
190	        self._fatal_lock = threading.Lock()
191	        self._queued_segments: set[int] = set()
192	        self._queued_lock = threading.Lock()
193	        if self.method != "none":
194	            self.bucket, self.prefix = parse_bos_uri(args.bos_prefix)
195	            if self.method in {"auto", "sdk"}:
196	                self.client = self._make_sdk_client()
197	                if self.client is not None:
198	                    self.method = "sdk"
199	                elif self.method == "sdk":
200	                    raise RuntimeError("BOS SDK requested but unavailable")
201	                else:
202	                    self.method = "bcecmd"
203	        self.workers = [
204	            threading.Thread(target=self._worker, name=f"bos-upload-{i}", daemon=True)
205	            for i in range(args.upload_workers)
206	        ]
207	        for t in self.workers:
208	            t.start()
209	
210	    def _set_failed(self, message: str) -> None:
211	        with self._fatal_lock:
212	            if self._fatal_error is None:
213	                self._fatal_error = message
214	
215	    def has_failed(self) -> bool:
216	        with self._fatal_lock:
217	            return self._fatal_error is not None
218	
219	    def raise_if_failed(self) -> None:
220	        with self._fatal_lock:
221	            if self._fatal_error is not None:
222	                raise RuntimeError(self._fatal_error)
223	
224	    def _make_sdk_client(self):
225	        try:
226	            from baidubce.auth.bce_credentials import BceCredentials
227	            from baidubce.bce_client_configuration import BceClientConfiguration
228	            from baidubce.services.bos.bos_client import BosClient
229	        except Exception:
230	            return None
231	        cred_path = Path(self.args.bce_conf) / "credentials"
232	        conf_path = Path(self.args.bce_conf) / "config"
233	        if not cred_path.exists():
234	            return None
235	        cp = configparser.ConfigParser()
236	        cp.read(cred_path)
237	        section = "Defaults" if cp.has_section("Defaults") else cp.sections()[0]
238	        ak = cp.get(section, "Ak", fallback=cp.get(section, "ak", fallback=""))
239	        sk = cp.get(section, "Sk", fallback=cp.get(section, "sk", fallback=""))
240	        if not ak or not sk:
241	            return None
242	        endpoint = "http://bj.bcebos.com"
243	        if conf_path.exists():
244	            cp2 = configparser.ConfigParser()
245	            cp2.read(conf_path)
246	            sec2 = "Defaults" if cp2.has_section("Defaults") else (cp2.sections()[0] if cp2.sections() else "")
247	            domain = cp2.get(sec2, "Domain", fallback="").strip()
248	            if domain:
249	                endpoint = "http://" + domain.replace("http://", "").replace("https://", "")
250	        return BosClient(BceClientConfiguration(
251	            credentials=BceCredentials(ak, sk),
252	            endpoint=endpoint,
253	        ))
254	
255	    def pending(self) -> int:
256	        return self.queue.qsize()
257	
258	    def submit(self, seg_dir: Path, seg_idx: int) -> None:
259	        if self.method == "none":
260	            return
261	        self.raise_if_failed()
262	        with self._queued_lock:
263	            if seg_idx in self.state.uploaded_segments or seg_idx in self._queued_segments:
264	                return
265	            self._queued_segments.add(seg_idx)
266	        self.queue.put((seg_dir, seg_idx))
267	
268	    def _worker(self) -> None:
269	        while not self.stop.is_set():
270	            try:
271	                item = self.queue.get(timeout=1.0)
272	            except Empty:
273	                continue
274	            if item is None:
275	                self.queue.task_done()
276	                break
277	            seg_dir, seg_idx = item
278	            try:
279	                self._upload_segment(seg_dir, seg_idx)
280	            except Exception as e:
281	                self._set_failed(
282	                    f"upload failed for seg_{seg_idx:06d}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}"
283	                )
284	            finally:
285	                with self._queued_lock:
286	                    self._queued_segments.discard(seg_idx)
287	                self.queue.task_done()
288	
289	    def _upload_segment(self, seg_dir: Path, seg_idx: int) -> None:
290	        if seg_idx in self.state.uploaded_segments:
291	            shutil.rmtree(seg_dir, ignore_errors=True)
292	            return
293	        files = sorted(seg_dir.glob("*.pt"))
294	        if not files:
295	            shutil.rmtree(seg_dir, ignore_errors=True)
296	            return
297	        manifest = self._write_success_manifest(seg_dir, seg_idx, files)
298	        n_bytes = int(manifest["bytes"])
299	        t0 = time.monotonic()
300	        if self.method == "sdk":
301	            self._upload_segment_sdk(seg_dir, seg_idx, files)
302	        else:
303	            self._upload_segment_bcecmd(seg_dir, seg_idx)
304	        elapsed = max(time.monotonic() - t0, 1e-6)
305	        self.state.mark_uploaded(seg_idx, len(files), n_bytes)
306	        with self._stats_lock:
307	            self.uploaded_bytes_this_run += n_bytes
308	            self.uploaded_files_this_run += len(files)
309	            self.upload_seconds_this_run += elapsed
310	        metric = {
311	            "type": "upload_segment",
312	            "segment": seg_idx,
313	            "files": len(files),
314	            "bytes": n_bytes,
315	            "elapsed_s": elapsed,
316	            "mb_s": n_bytes / elapsed / 1024**2,
317	            "method": self.method,
318	            "time": time.time(),
319	        }
320	        append_jsonl(self.args.metrics_file, metric)
321	        shutil.rmtree(seg_dir, ignore_errors=True)
322	        if self.pbar is not None:
323	            self.pbar.update(len(files))
324	
325	    def _write_success_manifest(
326	        self, seg_dir: Path, seg_idx: int, files: list[Path]
327	    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
328	        entries = []
329	        total = 0
330	        for f in files:
331	            size = f.stat().st_size
332	            total += size
333	            item = {"name": f.name, "bytes": size}
334	            if self.args.sha256:
335	                item["sha256"] = sha256_file(f)
336	            entries.append(item)
337	        manifest = {
338	            "schema": "eagle_v3mix_segment_v1",
339	            "segment": seg_idx,
340	            "files": len(files),
341	            "bytes": total,
342	            "sha256": bool(self.args.sha256),
343	            "created_at": time.time(),
344	            "entries": entries,
345	        }
346	        (seg_dir / "_SUCCESS.json").write_text(
347	            json.dumps(manifest, ensure_ascii=False, indent=1),
348	            encoding="utf-8",
349	        )
350	        return manifest
```
