```
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Profile current best train step to identify the hottest GPU ops.
3	
4	Single-mode: best path = async builder + cpu pinned + main H2D + fused AdamW.
5	Use torch.profiler to capture 3 bench steps after a 2-step warmup; print
6	top ops by self CUDA time.
7	
8	Run: python3 eagle/training/sala_draft/profile_hot_ops.py
9	"""
10	from __future__ import annotations
11	
12	import os
13	import random
14	import sys
15	import time
16	from pathlib import Path
17	
18	REPO_user_4813494d = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]
19	sys.path.insert(0, str(REPO_user_4813494d))
20	
21	os.environ.setdefault("PYTORCH_CUDA_ALLOC_CONF", "expandable_segments:True")
22	os.environ.setdefault("EAGLE_DRAFT_TTT_STEPS", "3")
23	os.environ.setdefault("EAGLE_DRAFT_SEQ_LEN_MAX", "4096")
24	os.environ.setdefault("EAGLE_DRAFT_PACK", "1")
25	
26	import torch
27	import torch.profiler as tp
28	
29	from eagle.legacy.v2_v3 import train as v3train
30	from eagle.training.sala_draft import train as v4train
31	from eagle.training.sala_draft.packing import (
32	    PackedFileSampler, index_lengths, make_packed_batch,
33	)
34	
35	
36	def build_model_and_opt():
37	    embed_w, lm_head_w = v3train.load_embed_and_lm_head()
38	    model = v4train.Eagle3ModelV4(embed_w, lm_head_w).to(v4train.DEVICE).to(v4train.DTYPE)
39	    model.load_v4_vocab_cache(Path(v4train.VOCAB_CACHE_V4))
40	    v3train.init_mlp_from_target(model)
41	    model.train()
42	    decay, no_decay = [], []
43	    for name, p in model.named_parameters():
44	        if not p.requires_grad: continue
45	        (no_decay if (name.endswith(".bias") or name.endswith("norm.weight")) else decay).append(p)
46	    opt = torch.optim.AdamW(
47	        [{"params": decay, "weight_decay": 0.01},
48	         {"params": no_decay, "weight_decay": 0.0}],
49	        lr=5e-4, betas=(0.9, 0.95), fused=True,
50	    )
51	    return model, opt
52	
53	
54	def make_build(sampler, BS, S, T):
55	    def build_one():
56	        bin_dicts = []
57	        for _ in range(BS):
58	            bf = sampler.next_bin()
59	            ss = [v4train.load_sample_v4(f) for f in bf]
60	            bin_dicts.append(make_packed_batch(
61	                ss, max_len=S, ttt_steps=T, device="cpu",
62	                dtype=v4train.DTYPE, min_seg_len=64, pin_memory=False,
63	            ))
64	        out = {}
65	        for k in bin_dicts[0]:
66	            v0 = bin_dicts[0][k]
67	            if torch.is_tensor(v0):
68	                t = torch.cat([d[k] for d in bin_dicts], dim=0)
69	                if torch.cuda.is_available():
70	                    t = t.pin_memory()
71	                out[k] = t
72	            else:
73	                out[k] = v0
74	        return out
75	    return build_one
76	
77	
78	def to_cuda(batch):
79	    return {
80	        k: (v.to(v4train.DEVICE, non_blocking=True) if torch.is_tensor(v) else v)
81	        for k, v in batch.items()
82	    }
83	
84	
85	def main():
86	    print(f"PyTorch {torch.__version__} on {torch.cuda.get_device_name(0)}")
87	    BS, S, T, GA = 4, 4096, 3, 4
88	    print(f"config: BS={BS} GA={GA} S={S} T={T}  effective={BS*GA}\n")
89	
90	    model, opt = build_model_and_opt()
91	
92	    data_dir = Path("eagle/data/target_regen/v2mix_20k")
93	    files = sorted(data_dir.glob("*.pt"))
94	    lengths = index_lengths(data_dir, data_dir.parent / f"{data_dir.name}.lengths.json", verbose=False)
95	    sampler = PackedFileSampler(files=files, lengths=lengths, max_len=S, min_seg_len=64,
96	                                ttt_pad=T, rng=random.Random(0))
97	    build_one = make_build(sampler, BS, S, T)
98	    pf = v4train._BatchPrefetcher(build_one, queue_size=2,
99	                                  on_error=lambda e: print(f"prefetch err: {e}"))
100	
101	    try:
102	        # Warmup (no profile)
103	        for _ in range(2):
104	            opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
105	            for _ in range(GA):
106	                batch = pf.next_batch()
107	                if batch is None: continue
108	                batch = to_cuda(batch)
109	                loss, _, _ = model(**batch)
110	                (loss / GA).backward()
111	            opt.step()
112	        torch.cuda.synchronize()
113	
114	        # Profile 3 steps
115	        print("[profile] capturing 3 step traces …")
116	        with tp.profile(
117	            activities=[tp.ProfilerActivity.CPU, tp.ProfilerActivity.CUDA],
118	            record_shapes=False, with_stack=False,
119	        ) as prof:
120	            for _ in range(3):
121	                opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
122	                for _ in range(GA):
123	                    batch = pf.next_batch()
124	                    if batch is None: continue
125	                    batch = to_cuda(batch)
126	                    loss, _, _ = model(**batch)
127	                    (loss / GA).backward()
128	                opt.step()
129	            torch.cuda.synchronize()
130	    finally:
131	        pf.close()
132	
133	    print("\n=== top 20 ops by self CUDA time ===")
134	    print(prof.key_averages().table(sort_by="cuda_time_total", row_limit=20))
135	
136	
137	if __name__ == "__main__":
138	    main()
139
```

```
4	This is the v3mix production collector:

38	def load_jsonl(path: Path, start: int, limit: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
39	    out = []
40	    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as f:
41	        for i, line in enumerate(f):
42	            if i < start or not line.strip():
43	                continue
44	            out.append(json.loads(line))
45	            if limit > 0 and len(out) >= limit:
46	                break
47	    return out

56	    return seg_dir / f"{idx:07d}_{source}.pt"

59	def segment_dir_for_idx(stage_dir: Path, idx: int, segment_size: int) -> tuple[int, Path]:

64	def disk_free_gb(path: Path) -> float:
65	    target = path
66	    while not target.exists() and target != target.parent:
67	        target = target.parent
68	    st = os.statvfs(target)
69	    return st.f_bavail * st.f_frsize / 1024**3

71	
72	def is_full_ignore_eos(idx: int, ratio: float) -> bool:
73	    if ratio <= 0:
74	        return False
75	    if ratio >= 1:
76	        return True
77	    return ((idx * 2654435761 % 10000) / 10000.0) < ratio

82	        raise ValueError(f"invalid BOS uri: {uri}")

84	    bucket, _, prefix = rest.partition("/")

93	def append_jsonl(path: Path, record: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
94	    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

99	def sha256_file(path: Path, block_size: int = 8 << 20) -> str:
100	    h = hashlib.sha256()
101	    with path.open("rb") as f:
102	        while True:
103	            chunk = f.read(block_size)
104	            if not chunk:
105	                break
106	            h.update(chunk)
107	    return h.hexdigest()

112	) -> dict[str, Any]:
113	    if isinstance(reason, (str, int, float, bool)) or reason is None:

115	    else:
116	        value = repr(reason)
117	    record = {"idx": int(idx), "reason": value, "time": time.time()}

128	class State:

134	        self.n_uploaded_files = 0

139	    def load(self) -> None:
140	        if not self.path.exists():
141	            return
142	        try:
143	            d = json.loads(self.path.read_text())
144	            self.next_idx = int(d.get("next_idx", 0))
145	            self.next_segment_idx = int(d.get("next_segment_idx", 0))
146	            self.uploaded_segments = set(map(int, d.get("uploaded_segments", [])))
147	            self.n_uploaded_files = int(d.get("n_uploaded_files", 0))
148	            self.n_uploaded_bytes = int(d.get("n_uploaded_bytes", 0))
149	        except Exception as e:

152	    def save(self) -> None:
153	        with self._lock:
154	            self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
155	            tmp = self.path.with_suffix(self.path.suffix + ".tmp")
156	            tmp.write_text(json.dumps({

158	                "next_segment_idx": self.next_segment_idx,
159	                "uploaded_segments": sorted(self.uploaded_segments),
160	                "n_uploaded_files": self.n_uploaded_files,
161	                "n_uploaded_bytes": self.n_uploaded_bytes,
162	                "updated_at": time.time(),
163	            }, indent=1))
164	            tmp.replace(self.path)

169	            self.n_uploaded_files += n_files
170	            self.n_uploaded_bytes += n_bytes
171	        self.save()
```

```
1	"""Sequence packing for SALA EAGLE-3 draft training.
2	
3	Pack multiple variable-length samples into one fixed-budget sequence to
4	eliminate padding waste. Each segment is fully isolated:
5	
6	  * Per-segment ``document_ids`` (0, 1, 2, ...). Cross-doc attention mask in
7	    the trainer blocks attention across segment boundaries.
8	  * Per-segment AOI sink + offset, so each segment's RoPE positions look
9	    independent (no global anchor on a packed sequence).
10	  * Per-segment tail masking. The trainer's TTT step k>0 left-shifts inputs
11	    by ``k`` positions, which on packed sequences pulls tokens from the
12	    next segment into the current segment's tail. We zero out the
13	    ``assistant_mask`` for the last ``ttt_steps`` positions of each segment
14	    so those polluted positions do not contribute to the loss.
15	
16	Public surface:
17	    index_lengths(data_dir, cache_path, force=False)
18	    pack_samples(samples, max_len, min_seg_len=64)
19	    aoi_position_ids_packed(seq_len, batch_size, document_ids, device,
20	                            training, aoi_cap, aoi_max_sink)
21	    mask_segment_tail(assistant_mask, document_ids, n_pad)
22	    make_packed_batch(samples, max_len, ttt_steps, device, dtype, min_seg_len=64)
23	    PackedFileSampler(files, lengths, max_len, min_seg_len)
24	"""
25	from __future__ import annotations
26	
27	import json
28	import multiprocessing as mp
29	import os
30	import random
31	import time
32	from pathlib import Path
33	
34	import torch
35	
36	
37	# ── Length index ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
38	
39	def _index_one(path_str: str) -> tuple[str, int | None, str | None]:
40	    """Single-file worker: returns (name, n_tokens, error_or_None).
41	
42	    Defined at module top level so multiprocessing.Pool can pickle it.
43	    """
44	    p = Path(path_str)
45	    try:
46	        d = torch.load(p, weights_only=True, map_location="cpu")
47	        return p.name, int(d["token_ids"].shape[0]), None
48	    except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001
49	        return p.name, None, f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"
50	
51	
52	def index_lengths(data_dir: Path, cache_path: Path,
53	                  force: bool = False, verbose: bool = True,
54	                  num_workers: int | None = None) -> dict[str, int]:
55	    """Build {filename: token_ids length} index, persisted as JSON.
56	
57	    Loads every .pt once and records its ``token_ids`` length. Uses a
58	    multiprocessing pool to parallelize the torch.load — single-process
59	    runs at ~50 file/s due to I/O, multi-process scales linearly until
60	    storage saturates. Subsequent calls short-circuit on the cache; new
61	    files get filled in incrementally.
62	
63	    num_workers default = min(32, os.cpu_count() // 2). The bottleneck is
64	    storage IOPS, not CPU; oversubscribing has no benefit.
65	    """
66	    cache_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
67	    cached: dict[str, int] = {}
68	    if cache_path.exists() and not force:
69	        try:
70	            cached = json.loads(cache_path.read_text())
71	        except Exception:
72	            cached = {}
73	
74	    files = sorted(data_dir.glob("*.pt"))
75	    missing = [f for f in files if f.name not in cached]
76	    if not missing:
77	        return cached
78	
79	    if num_workers is None:
80	        num_workers = min(32, max(2, (os.cpu_count() or 4) // 2))
81	
82	    if verbose:
83	        print(f"[packing.index_lengths] {data_dir}: {len(missing)} new files to scan "
84	              f"({len(cached)} cached, {len(files)} total, workers={num_workers})")
85	    t0 = time.time()
86	    n_done = 0
87	    n_skip = 0
88	
89	    # fork is fine here: workers only do torch.load(map_location="cpu") and
90	    # never touch CUDA, so fork-time CUDA state inheritance is harmless. spawn
91	    # would re-import the parent's __main__ (which tries to rebuild the full
92	    # model + optimizer in every worker on every TTT step — many minutes lost
93	    # before the first index even completes).
94	    ctx = mp.get_context("fork")
95	    chunksize = max(1, len(missing) // (num_workers * 16) or 1)
96	    paths = [str(f) for f in missing]
97	
98	    with ctx.Pool(processes=num_workers) as pool:
99	        for name, n, err in pool.imap_unordered(_index_one, paths, chunksize=chunksize):
100	            n_done += 1
101	            if n is None:
102	                n_skip += 1
103	                if verbose:
104	                    print(f"  skip {name}: {err}")
105	                continue
106	            cached[name] = n
107	            if verbose and n_done % 1000 == 0:
108	                rate = n_done / (time.time() - t0)
109	                print(f"  scanned {n_done}/{len(missing)} ({time.time()-t0:.1f}s, {rate:.0f}/s)")
110	
111	    cache_path.write_text(json.dumps(cached))
112	    if verbose:
113	        print(f"[packing.index_lengths] wrote {cache_path} ({len(cached)} entries, "
114	              f"{n_skip} skipped, {time.time()-t0:.1f}s)")
115	    return cached
116	
117	
118	# ── Pack one bin from a list of loaded samples ─────────────────────────
119	
120	PACK_KEYS = (
121	    "token_ids",
122	    "aux_hidden",
123	    "top_logit_values",
124	    "top_logit_indices",
125	    "target_logsumexp",
126	    "assistant_mask",
127	)
128	
129	
130	def pack_samples(samples: list[dict], max_len: int,
131	                 min_seg_len: int = 64) -> dict | None:
132	    """Concatenate already-loaded samples along the seq axis.
133	
134	    Returns one packed dict (single-row, no batch dim) or None if no
135	    segment is admissible. Segments shorter than ``min_seg_len`` are
136	    silently dropped (they cannot afford the TTT tail masking).
137	
138	    Stops adding segments once the running length would exceed ``max_len``.
139	    The caller (bin packer) is responsible for choosing which samples to
140	    feed in; this routine does not reorder.
141	    """
142	    pieces = []
143	    total = 0
144	    for s in samples:
145	        n = int(s["token_ids"].shape[0])
146	        if n < min_seg_len:
147	            continue
148	        if total + n > max_len:
149	            continue
150	        pieces.append((s, n))
```
```

250	
251	    def __init__(self, files: list[Path], lengths: dict[str, int],
252	                 max_len: int, min_seg_len: int = 64,
253	                 ttt_pad: int = 0, rng: random.Random | None = None):
254	        self.max_len = max_len
255	        self.min_seg_len = min_seg_len
256	        self.ttt_pad = ttt_pad
257	        self.rng = rng or random
258	        self._all = [
259	            (p, int(lengths[p.name]))
260	            for p in files
261	            if p.name in lengths and int(lengths[p.name]) >= min_seg_len
262	        ]
263	        if not self._all:
264	            raise RuntimeError("PackedFileSampler: no files >= min_seg_len after indexing")
265	        self._reset_pool()
266	
267	    def _reset_pool(self):
268	        pool = list(self._all)
269	        self.rng.shuffle(pool)
270	        # Sort by length descending — FFD prefers placing the largest first.
271	        pool.sort(key=lambda x: -x[1])
272	        self._pool = pool
273	
274	    def stats(self) -> dict:
275	        lens = [n for _, n in self._all]
276	        return {
277	            "n_files": len(self._all),
278	            "min_len": min(lens),
279	            "max_len": max(lens),
280	            "mean_len": sum(lens) / len(lens),
281	            "pool_remaining": len(self._pool),
282	        }
283	
284	    def next_bin(self) -> list[Path]:
285	        """Return file paths for one packed batch. Reshuffles when the pool
286	        empties without ever returning an empty bin (raises only if no
287	        single file fits — which would mean min_seg_len > max_len).
288	        """
289	        bin_files: list[Path] = []
290	        bin_filled = 0
291	        # Walk the pool once looking for files that fit.
292	        kept_indices: list[int] = []
293	        for i, (p, n) in enumerate(self._pool):
294	            if bin_filled + n <= self.max_len:
295	                bin_files.append(p)
296	                bin_filled += n
297	            else:
298	                kept_indices.append(i)
299	        # Compute the survivors (everything we didn't consume).
300	        new_pool = [self._pool[i] for i in kept_indices]
301	        # If the pool actually became smaller, this round made progress.
302	        if len(new_pool) < len(self._pool):
303	            self._pool = new_pool
304	        else:
305	            # Nothing fit (e.g. all remaining files exceed max_len).
306	            # Drop the first (largest leftover) so we don't loop.
307	            self._pool = self._pool[1:]
308	        if not self._pool:
309	            self._reset_pool()
310	        if not bin_files:
311	            # Single file exceeds max_len: take the smallest one anyway.
312	            small = min(self._all, key=lambda x: x[1])
313	            bin_files = [small[0]]
314	        return bin_files
315	
316	
317	# ── Top-level batch builder ────────────────────────────────────────────
318	
319	def make_packed_batch(samples: list[dict], max_len: int, ttt_steps: int,
320	                      device: str | torch.device, dtype: torch.dtype,
321	                      min_seg_len: int = 64,
322	                      pin_memory: bool = False) -> dict:
323	    """Build a (B=1) packed batch.
324	
325	    Tail masking is applied to ``assistant_mask`` on whichever device we
326	    end on. ``pin_memory=True`` is honored only when device==cpu (CUDA
327	    tensors are already in device memory).
328	
329	    Stream-separation pattern: caller passes ``device='cpu', pin_memory=True``
330	    inside a worker thread, then in the main thread does
331	    ``{k: v.to('cuda', non_blocking=True) for k, v in batch.items()}`` so
332	    H2D copy runs on PyTorch's default copy stream and overlaps with
333	    forward compute on the main stream.
334	    """
335	    packed = pack_samples(samples, max_len, min_seg_len=min_seg_len)
336	    if packed is None:
337	        raise RuntimeError("make_packed_batch: pack_samples returned None")
338	
339	    n_real = packed["token_ids"].shape[0]
340	    if n_real < max_len:
341	        pad_len = max_len - n_real
342	        for k, t in list(packed.items()):
343	            if k.startswith("_") or not torch.is_tensor(t):
344	                continue
345	            pad_shape = list(t.shape)
346	            pad_shape[0] = pad_len
347	            pad_value = 0
348	            pad = torch.full(pad_shape, pad_value, dtype=t.dtype)
349	            packed[k] = torch.cat([t, pad], dim=0)
350	        pad_doc = int(packed["document_ids"][n_real - 1].item()) + 1
351	        packed["document_ids"][n_real:] = pad_doc
352	        packed["assistant_mask"][n_real:] = False
353	
354	    # Build batch on CPU first (cheap dtype casts only), then move/pin.
355	    out = {
356	        "token_ids": packed["token_ids"].unsqueeze(0).long(),
357	        "aux_hidden": packed["aux_hidden"].unsqueeze(0).to(dtype),
358	        "target_logits_values": packed["top_logit_values"].unsqueeze(0).to(torch.float32),
359	        "target_logits_indices": packed["top_logit_indices"].unsqueeze(0).long(),
360	        "target_logsumexp": packed["target_logsumexp"].unsqueeze(0).to(torch.float32),
361	        "assistant_mask": packed["assistant_mask"].unsqueeze(0).bool(),
362	        "document_ids": packed["document_ids"].unsqueeze(0).long(),
363	        "is_packed": True,
364	    }
365	    # Apply TTT tail masking on CPU before the H2D copy so the GPU never
366	    # sees an unmasked tail (and so we can keep the .to() unconditional).
367	    out["assistant_mask"] = mask_segment_tail(
368	        out["assistant_mask"], out["document_ids"], n_pad=ttt_steps
369	    )
370	
371	    is_cpu_target = (str(device) == "cpu")
372	    if pin_memory and is_cpu_target and torch.cuda.is_available():
373	        for k, v in list(out.items()):
374	            if torch.is_tensor(v):
375	                out[k] = v.pin_memory()
376	    elif not is_cpu_target:
377	        for k, v in list(out.items()):
378	            if torch.is_tensor(v):
379	                out[k] = v.to(device, non_blocking=True)
380	    return out
381
```

```
def next_files(n: int) -> list[Path]:
        nonlocal file_pos, files
        out = []
        while len(out) < n:
            if file_pos >= len(files):
                random.shuffle(files)
                file_pos = 0
            out.append(files[file_pos])
            file_pos += 1
        return out

    def run_eval(step: int):
        nonlocal best_ood0, best_step

        )
        ood_accs = evaluate_v4(

        )
```

```
1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
2	"""Convert a SALA EAGLE checkpoint into an SGLang draft model directory."""
3	
4	from __future__ import annotations

38	def _get_state_weight(state: dict[str, torch.Tensor], key: str) -> torch.Tensor:

44	def _load_v4_d2t_diff(vocab_cache_path: Path) -> torch.Tensor:
45	    cache = torch.load(vocab_cache_path, weights_only=True, map_location="cpu")
46	    if "selected_ids" in cache:
47	        selected_ids = cache["selected_ids"].long()
48	        if selected_ids.numel() != DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE:
49	            raise ValueError(
50	                f"selected_ids has {selected_ids.numel()} entries, expected {DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE}"
51	            )
52	        return selected_ids - torch.arange(DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE, dtype=torch.long)
53	
54	    d2t = cache["d2t"].long()
55	    if d2t.numel() != DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE:
56	        raise ValueError(f"d2t has {d2t.numel()} entries, expected {DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE}")
57	    # v4 cache stores diff-format d2t. Keep this branch for robustness if the
58	    # cache is regenerated without selected_ids.
59	    if int(d2t.max()) < VOCAB_SIZE and int(d2t.min()) >= -DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE:
60	        return d2t
61	    return d2t - torch.arange(DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE, dtype=torch.long)
```

```
from typing import Any

from tqdm import tqdm

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]))
from eagle import nvfp4_codec


API_BASE = "http://127.0.0.1:30000"


def load_jsonl(path: Path, start: int, limit: int) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    out = []
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8") as f:
        for i, line in enumerate(f):
            if i < start or not line.strip():
                continue
            out.append(json.loads(line))
            if limit > 0 and len(out) >= limit:
                break
    return out


def safe_name(s: str) -> str:
```

### 任务 1：`add_nvfp4_tensors()` 完整实现

**文件位置：** `/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/legacy/v2_v3/convert_to_sglang.py` (行 134-154)

```python
def add_nvfp4_tensors(
    tensors: dict,
    sglang_key: str,
    weight_bf16: torch.Tensor,
    num_logical_weights: int | None = None,
) -> None:
    """Pack weight and add the three NVFP4 tensors to the output dict."""
    packed, scale, scale2 = pack_nvfp4(weight_bf16)
    tensors[f"{sglang_key}.weight"]       = packed
    tensors[f"{sglang_key}.weight_scale"] = scale
    if num_logical_weights is not None:
        assert num_logical_weights > 0, f"invalid num_logical_weights={num_logical_weights}"
        scale2 = scale2.reshape(1).repeat(num_logical_weights)
        input_scale = torch.ones(num_logical_weights, dtype=torch.float32)
    else:
        input_scale = torch.tensor(1.0, dtype=torch.float32)
    tensors[f"{sglang_key}.weight_scale_2"] = scale2
    tensors[f"{sglang_key}.input_scale"] = input_scale
```

**依赖关系：**
- 内部调用 `pack_nvfp4()` (同文件 line 82)，仅依赖标准库：`torch`, `safetensors.torch`, `json`, `pathlib`, `os`
- 无外部量化库（sgl_kernel、flashinfer）依赖
- 量化逻辑完全自包含：FP4 E2M1 值表、bucketize 舍入、uint8 nibble 打包、FP8 全局缩放均在 convert_to_sglang.py 中实现

**可搬迁性：** ✓ 独立，无其他 import 依赖

   - `build_vocab_mapping(data_dir)` 从训练数据构建 draft vocab 并初始化 lm_head
   - `_make_causal_mask(total_len, device)` → (1, 1, S, S) causal mask

### 任务 5：流式下 file pool 增量补充对 FFD packer 随机性的影响

**关键代码分析：**

**`next_files()` (train.py line 902-911)：**
```python
def next_files(n: int) -> list[Path]:
    nonlocal file_pos, files
    out = []
    while len(out) < n:
        if file_pos >= len(files):
            random.shuffle(files)  # ← 1 epoch shuffle
            file_pos = 0
        out.append(files[file_pos])
        file_pos += 1
    return out
```

**`PackedFileSampler._reset_pool()` (packing.py line 267-272)：**
```python
def _reset_pool(self):
    pool = list(self._all)
    self.rng.shuffle(pool)
    # Sort by length descending — FFD prefers placing the largest first.
    pool.sort(key=lambda x: -x[1])  # ← 贪心排序
    self._pool = pool
```

**FFD packing 随机性变化分析：**

1. **当前状态（批量加载）：** 每 epoch 一次 shuffle，FFD 在**确定的长度排序**下贪心装箱
   - 随机性仅来自文件顺序的 1 次 shuffle
   - 同一 epoch 内多个 bin 的装箱结果**完全确定**

2. **流式增量补充下：** 若 segment-by-segment 下载完成就 append 到 pool：
   - `_reset_pool()` 的 shuffle + sort 会多次触发（每次 pool 耗尽）
   - FFD 贪心排序始终相同（按长度），但待选文件集合**动态变化**
   - 同一 batch 在不同时刻可能选中不同的文件（如果恰好在重 shuffle 时刻请求）
   - **结果：** 随机性增加，但仍可控（rng 种子确定则可复现）

3. **`_reset_pool()` 语义改动需求：**
   - 若要维持 batch-to-batch 一致性，需将**增量补充**与**packer 请求**解耦
   - 建议：pool 补充不触发 reset，仅在 `next_bin()` 返回空时才 reset，并用版本号/timestamp 标记 pool 世代
   - **不改的影响：** 不同运行/不同时刻启动的训练会看到不同的数据打包方式（可能影响可复现性，但不影响收敛）

## 设计要求

请输出实施方案，覆盖以下五块。**不要给替选方案，只给推荐方案**。重点是清晰的步骤和文件清单。

### 块 A: 切断 legacy import + 目录重整

### 块 B: bin/ 清理 + 新脚本
- 列出 `bin/` 的最终状态（删/归档/保留/新增），每个文件一行说明
- 新增的 `bin/train.sh` 默认参数（TTT=7 BS=4 GA=4 PACK=1，路径全对齐到新命名）
- 新增 `bin/stream_train.sh`（流式下载 + 训练联动，这是块 D 的入口）

### 块 C: train.py 默认值修正 + 数据递归 glob
- DATA_DIR / VOCAB_CACHE_V4 / OUTPUT_DIR 改成什么？
- `glob("*.pt")` 不递归怎么处理？我倾向"下载脚本里建 flat 软链"，但需要论证为啥不直接改 `glob("**/*.pt")`（PackedFileSampler.lengths.json 缓存 key 是 filename，重名风险？）

### 块 D: 流式 BOS 拉取 → 训练 pipeline（最核心）

**目标**：训练启动后台 N 个 bcecmd worker 并行下载 segment，train 侧的 PackedFileSampler 动态消费已就绪 file，segment 全部消费完即 rm 释放空间。本地常驻 < 50 GB（不是 3.4 TiB）。

**设计点**：
1. **下载 worker 池**：8 个并发，每个 worker pop 一个 segment idx → `bcecmd sync` → 写 sentinel `.ready` → 通知 sampler
2. **shuffle 策略**：启动时先对 4688 个 segment idx 做一次全局 shuffle（rng seed），按这个顺序入下载队列。Segment 内 64 file 由 PackedFileSampler 二次 shuffle。
3. **PackedFileSampler 增量补充**：现有 `_all` 是一次性扫描的静态 list。要改成 `_dynamic_all` + 一把锁，下载 worker 把新 file 信息（path + length）push 进来。`next_bin` 在 pool 空时不重新 shuffle 整个 `_all`，而是从最新的"待消费 file 集合"贪心装箱。**file length 怎么拿**：在 download worker 完成一个 segment 后启动一个轻量 indexing thread 跑 torch.load(weights_only=True) 取 token_ids.shape[0]，写入 in-memory dict（不写 lengths.json 文件，因为 file 是临时的）。
4. **空间管理**：训练完一个 file 后**不**立刻删（segment 内可能有其他还没训的 file）。维护 `segment → consumed_files` 计数，等一个 segment 64 个 file 全 consumed → rmtree。
5. **背压**：若已就绪 segment 数 > 阈值（如 20，即 ~15 GB 数据 buffered），下载 worker 暂停 — 防训练慢于下载时把磁盘撑爆。若已就绪 segment 数 < 阈值（如 4，即 ~3 GB），训练 sampler 阻塞等待 — 防 starving。
6. **错误处理**：下载失败重试 3 次；index 失败标记 segment bad 跳过；训练侧 sampler 看到 file 数量为 0 时 block + warn。

**两种实现路径选哪个**：
- 路径 1：完全独立的 `streaming_dataloader.py`，replace PackedFileSampler；与 train.py 解耦
- 路径 2：继承 PackedFileSampler，加一个 `StreamingPackedSampler`，最小侵入 train.py

我倾向路径 2（最小改动），但你判断。

### 块 E: 测试三连
1. **smoke train**：跑 30-50 step，验证 loss 单调下降（粗），数据流水线通畅，不 OOM
2. **few-samples overfit**：4 个 file 重复跑 200 step，期望 step0 acc → 1.0（验证模型/loss/优化器没 bug）
3. **streaming profile**：8 个 segment 模拟 3 个 segment 缓冲，跑 30-50 step，验证 step time 与非流式相当（TTT=3 baseline 4.604 s/step，streaming 不应该 > 5.0 s/step）

每个测试给 (a) 脚本路径和 entry point；(b) 期望输出/通过标准；(c) 失败时排查方向。

```
239	class PackedFileSampler:
240	    """First-fit-decreasing bin packer over a static file list.
241	
242	    Each call to ``next_bin()`` returns a list of file paths whose
243	    ``token_ids`` lengths fit a single packed sequence of ``max_len``.
244	    Files shorter than ``min_seg_len`` are dropped at index time.
245	
246	    Internally the sampler keeps a shuffled list of (path, length) pairs
247	    and consumes them in FFD greedy order. When the pool empties it
248	    reshuffles. This is randomized but reproducible across epochs.
249	    """
250	
251	    def __init__(self, files: list[Path], lengths: dict[str, int],
252	                 max_len: int, min_seg_len: int = 64,
253	                 ttt_pad: int = 0, rng: random.Random | None = None):
254	        self.max_len = max_len
255	        self.min_seg_len = min_seg_len
256	        self.ttt_pad = ttt_pad
257	        self.rng = rng or random
258	        self._all = [
259	            (p, int(lengths[p.name]))
260	            for p in files
261	            if p.name in lengths and int(lengths[p.name]) >= min_seg_len
262	        ]
263	        if not self._all:
264	            raise RuntimeError("PackedFileSampler: no files >= min_seg_len after indexing")
265	        self._reset_pool()
266	
267	    def _reset_pool(self):
268	        pool = list(self._all)
269	        self.rng.shuffle(pool)
270	        # Sort by length descending — FFD prefers placing the largest first.
271	        pool.sort(key=lambda x: -x[1])
272	        self._pool = pool
273	
274	    def stats(self) -> dict:
275	        lens = [n for _, n in self._all]
276	        return {
277	            "n_files": len(self._all),
278	            "min_len": min(lens),
279	            "max_len": max(lens),
280	            "mean_len": sum(lens) / len(lens),
281	            "pool_remaining": len(self._pool),
282	        }
283	
284	    def next_bin(self) -> list[Path]:
285	        """Return file paths for one packed batch. Reshuffles when the pool
286	        empties without ever returning an empty bin (raises only if no
287	        single file fits — which would mean min_seg_len > max_len).
288	        """
289	        bin_files: list[Path] = []
290	        bin_filled = 0
291	        # Walk the pool once looking for files that fit.
292	        kept_indices: list[int] = []
293	        for i, (p, n) in enumerate(self._pool):
294	            if bin_filled + n <= self.max_len:
295	                bin_files.append(p)
296	                bin_filled += n
297	            else:
298	                kept_indices.append(i)
299	        # Compute the survivors (everything we didn't consume).
300	        new_pool = [self._pool[i] for i in kept_indices]
301	        # If the pool actually became smaller, this round made progress.
302	        if len(new_pool) < len(self._pool):
303	            self._pool = new_pool
304	        else:
305	            # Nothing fit (e.g. all remaining files exceed max_len).
306	            # Drop the first (largest leftover) so we don't loop.
307	            self._pool = self._pool[1:]
308	        if not self._pool:
309	            self._reset_pool()
310	        if not bin_files:
311	            # Single file exceeds max_len: take the smallest one anyway.
312	            small = min(self._all, key=lambda x: x[1])
313	            bin_files = [small[0]]
314	        return bin_files
315	
316	
317	# ── Top-level batch builder ────────────────────────────────────────────
318	
319	def make_packed_batch(samples: list[dict], max_len: int, ttt_steps: int,
320	                      device: str | torch.device, dtype: torch.dtype,
321	                      min_seg_len: int = 64,
322	                      pin_memory: bool = False) -> dict:
323	    """Build a (B=1) packed batch.
324	
325	    Tail masking is applied to ``assistant_mask`` on whichever device we
326	    end on. ``pin_memory=True`` is honored only when device==cpu (CUDA
327	    tensors are already in device memory).
328	
329	    Stream-separation pattern: caller passes ``device='cpu', pin_memory=True``
330	    inside a worker thread, then in the main thread does
331	    ``{k: v.to('cuda', non_blocking=True) for k, v in batch.items()}`` so
332	    H2D copy runs on PyTorch's default copy stream and overlaps with
333	    forward compute on the main stream.
334	    """
335	    packed = pack_samples(samples, max_len, min_seg_len=min_seg_len)
336	    if packed is None:
337	        raise RuntimeError("make_packed_batch: pack_samples returned None")
338	
339	    n_real = packed["token_ids"].shape[0]
340	    if n_real < max_len:
341	        pad_len = max_len - n_real
342	        for k, t in list(packed.items()):
343	            if k.startswith("_") or not torch.is_tensor(t):
344	                continue
345	            pad_shape = list(t.shape)
346	            pad_shape[0] = pad_len
347	            pad_value = 0
348	            pad = torch.full(pad_shape, pad_value, dtype=t.dtype)
349	            packed[k] = torch.cat([t, pad], dim=0)
350	        pad_doc = int(packed["document_ids"][n_real - 1].item()) + 1
351	        packed["document_ids"][n_real:] = pad_doc
352	        packed["assistant_mask"][n_real:] = False
353	
354	    # Build batch on CPU first (cheap dtype casts only), then move/pin.
355	    out = {
356	        "token_ids": packed["token_ids"].unsqueeze(0).long(),
357	        "aux_hidden": packed["aux_hidden"].unsqueeze(0).to(dtype),
358	        "target_logits_values": packed["top_logit_values"].unsqueeze(0).to(torch.float32),
359	        "target_logits_indices": packed["top_logit_indices"].unsqueeze(0).long(),
360	        "target_logsumexp": packed["target_logsumexp"].unsqueeze(0).to(torch.float32),
361	        "assistant_mask": packed["assistant_mask"].unsqueeze(0).bool(),
362	        "document_ids": packed["document_ids"].unsqueeze(0).long(),
363	        "is_packed": True,
364	    }
365	    # Apply TTT tail masking on CPU before the H2D copy so the GPU never
366	    # sees an unmasked tail (and so we can keep the .to() unconditional).
367	    out["assistant_mask"] = mask_segment_tail(
368	        out["assistant_mask"], out["document_ids"], n_pad=ttt_steps
```

```
85	# ── EAGLE-3 Draft Model ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
86	class Eagle3Model(nn.Module):
87	    def __init__(self, embed_weight, lm_head_weight):
88	        """
89	        embed_weight: (vocab_size, hidden_size) from target model
90	        lm_head_weight: (vocab_size, hidden_size) from target model
91	        """
92	        super().__init__()
93	        # v3: fc stays bf16 (no FP4 QAT) — aux_hidden is already NVFP4-stored,
94	        # fp4-quantizing fc on top would double-quantize the adapter.
95	        # deploy side (convert_to_sglang.py) also keeps fc bf16.
96	        self.fc = nn.Linear(AUX_DIM, HIDDEN_SIZE, bias=False)
97	        self.midlayer = Eagle3DecoderLayer()
98	        self.norm = RMSNorm(HIDDEN_SIZE, RMS_NORM_EPS)
99	
100	        # Frozen embedding from target (with scale_emb)
101	        self.embed_tokens = nn.Embedding.from_pretrained(embed_weight, freeze=True)
102	        self.scale_emb = SCALE_EMB
103	
104	        # lm_head initialized from target (subset rows set in _init_lm_head)
105	        self.lm_head = FP4QATLinear(HIDDEN_SIZE, DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE, bias=False)
106	        # Vocab projection is the most accuracy-sensitive Linear. Keep bf16
107	        # by default (FP4 noise leaks into every token's softmax).
108	        if NVFP4_EXCLUDE_LM_HEAD:
109	            self.lm_head._fp4_skip = True
110	        self._full_lm_head_weight = lm_head_weight  # kept for init after vocab mapping
111	
112	        # d2t / t2d / draft_idx_map mappings (set later via build_vocab_mapping)
113	        self.register_buffer("d2t", torch.zeros(DRAFT_VOCAB_SIZE, dtype=torch.long))
114	        self.register_buffer("t2d", torch.zeros(VOCAB_SIZE, dtype=torch.bool))
115	        self.register_buffer("draft_idx_map", torch.zeros(VOCAB_SIZE, dtype=torch.long))
116	
117	        self.ttt_steps = TTT_STEPS
118	        self.loss_decay = LOSS_DECAY
119	
120	    def build_vocab_mapping(self, data_dir):
121	        """Build draft vocabulary from training data token frequency."""
122	        cache_path = data_dir.parent / "vocab_cache.pt"
123	        if cache_path.exists():
124	            cache = torch.load(cache_path, weights_only=True)
125	            self.d2t.copy_(cache["d2t"])
126	            self.t2d.copy_(cache["t2d"])
127	            print(f"Loaded vocab mapping from {cache_path}")
128	        else:
129	            print("Building draft vocabulary from training data...")
```

# 实施方案：legacy 切断 + 流式 BOS 管线 + 测试

设计原则：搬而不拆、改最少的现有代码、流式用 in-place 子类化、命名一次性对齐。总耗时预估 **2-2.5 天**（A:3h, B:1h, C:1h, D:6-8h, E:3h）。

---

## 块 A — 切断 legacy + 目录重整 (3h)

**直觉**：`v3train` 引用的 17 个符号里，常量该回归 `core/`，类/函数该平迁到 `training/sala_draft/`。不再拆 `_target_init.py`——`load_embed_and_lm_head` + `init_mlp_from_target` 加起来 ~90 行，留在 `model.py` 顶层就好；要走"自给自足"就别引入新的微模块。

**常量归位** — 补到 `/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/core/arch.py` 末尾（不开新文件）：
```python

SCALE_WIDTH = HIDDEN_SIZE / 256
TARGET_INIT_LAYER = 16

```

**新增文件**（3 个，不要 `_target_init.py`）：
- `/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/training/sala_draft/model.py` ← 搬 `Eagle3Model`（legacy:86-311，~225 行）+ `load_embed_and_lm_head`（404-441）+ `init_mlp_from_target`（444-493）+ `_load_sample/_collate_cpu/load_batch/AsyncPrefetcher`（324-402）。本文件只 import `eagle.core.{arch,fp4_quant}`，零 legacy 引用。
- `/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/training/sala_draft/convert_to_sglang.py` ← 把 `add_nvfp4_tensors`（legacy/convert_to_sglang.py:134-154，~20 行）整段内联到此文件顶部，删除 `from eagle.legacy...` 行。
- `/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/training/sala_draft/train.py` 改：第 89 行删 `from eagle.legacy.v2_v3 import train as v3train`；110-121 行的 17 个 `HIDDEN_SIZE = v3train.HIDDEN_SIZE` 风格 alias 全删，直接 `from eagle.core.arch import HIDDEN_SIZE, ...`；`v3train.Eagle3Model` → `from .model import Eagle3Model`；`v3train.load_embed_and_lm_head/init_mlp_from_target/fp4_quant_freeze/_build_rope_cache` 全部走 `eagle.core` 或 `.model`。

**验证**：`grep -r "eagle\.legacy\|v3train" /user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/training/ /user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/core/` 必须为空。

## 块 C — train.py 默认值 + 递归 glob (1h)

**直觉**：默认值改成真实路径，glob 不改递归——保持 flat 假设，因为流式下载和静态训练**都**会走 flat 目录（流式直接平铺写本地、静态走 symlink 平铺）。

**train.py:100-104 改为**：
```python
DATA_DIR = Path("/user_4813494d/data/eagle_stage/main")
VAL_IND_DIR = Path("/user_4813494d/data/eagle_stage/val_ind")
VAL_OOD_DIR = Path("/user_4813494d/data/eagle_stage/val_ood")
VOCAB_CACHE_V4 = Path("/user_4813494d/data/eagle_stage/vocab_main.pt")
OUTPUT_DIR = Path("/user_4813494d/data/eagle_stage/weights/draft_main")
```

**为什么不改 `glob("**/*.pt")`**：`PackedFileSampler.__init__` 索引 file 时 `lengths[p.name]`（packing.py:259），key 是裸 filename。BOS 数据 `0000123_dapo_math.pt` 这类前缀在 4688 个 segment 间**可能重名**（每 segment 都从 0 开始编号）。改递归会让 lengths.json key collision。流式管线本就需要扁平命名（segment 内消费完即 rmtree），所以下载脚本里把文件写成 `seg<XXXXXX>_<orig_filename>` 解 collision；静态训练沿用 v3mix_smoke_256_real 那套 flat 目录即可。

## 块 D — 流式 BOS → 训练 pipeline (6-8h，核心)

**直觉**：路径 2（继承 PackedFileSampler）。新建一个 `streaming.py` 放 downloader/buffer manager，`StreamingPackedSampler` 在 `packing.py` 末尾追加 ~80 行子类。train.py 只动 30 行（加 `--streaming` 分支）。

### D.1 新增文件 `/user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/training/sala_draft/streaming.py`（~250 行）

**核心数据结构**：
```python
@dataclass
class SegmentState:
    idx: int                    # 0..4687
    local_dir: Path             # /user_4813494d/data/eagle_stage/stream/seg_NNNNNN
    files: list[Path]           # populated post-download
    file_lengths: dict[str, int]  # filename -> n_tokens
    consumed: set[str]          # filenames train side has finished
    status: str                 # 'queued' | 'downloading' | 'ready' | 'consumed'

class StreamBuffer:
    """Single shared state between downloader pool and sampler."""
    def __init__(self, total_segments: int, max_buffered: int, min_buffered: int,
                 stream_user_4813494d: Path, rng: random.Random):
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self.cv_buffer_full = threading.Condition(self.lock)   # downloader waits
        self.cv_buffer_low = threading.Condition(self.lock)    # sampler waits
        self.queue = collections.deque(rng.sample(range(total_segments), total_segments))
        self.ready: dict[int, SegmentState] = {}
        self.in_flight: set[int] = set()
        self.max_buffered = max_buffered  # 12
        self.min_buffered = min_buffered  # 3
        self.stream_user_4813494d = stream_user_4813494d
        self.done_count = 0
        self.total = total_segments
```

**Downloader worker**（8 个 thread）：
```python
def _download_worker(buf: StreamBuffer, bos_prefix: str, worker_id: int):
    while True:
        with buf.lock:
            # backpressure: pause if buffer above HWM
            while len(buf.ready) >= buf.max_buffered:
                buf.cv_buffer_full.wait()
            if not buf.queue:
                return
            seg_idx = buf.queue.popleft()
            buf.in_flight.add(seg_idx)
        seg_remote = f"{bos_prefix}/seg_{seg_idx:06d}/"
        seg_local = buf.stream_user_4813494d / f"seg_{seg_idx:06d}"
        seg_local.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        for attempt in range(3):
            r = subprocess.run(["bcecmd", "bos", "sync", seg_remote, str(seg_local)],
                               capture_output=True, timeout=600)
            if r.returncode == 0: break
        else:
            shutil.rmtree(seg_local, ignore_errors=True)
            with buf.lock: buf.in_flight.discard(seg_idx); buf.done_count += 1
            continue
        # rename to dedupe: 0000123_xxx.pt -> seg<NNNNNN>_0000123_xxx.pt
        files = []
        for p in seg_local.glob("*.pt"):
            new = seg_local / f"seg{seg_idx:06d}_{p.name}"
            p.rename(new); files.append(new)
        # index lengths inline (single-thread torch.load, ~50 ms/file × 64 = 3 s)
        lens = {}
        for p in files:
            try:
                obj = torch.load(p, weights_only=True, map_location='cpu')
                lens[p.name] = int(obj["token_ids"].shape[0])
            except Exception: continue
        with buf.lock:
            buf.ready[seg_idx] = SegmentState(seg_idx, seg_local, files, lens, set(), "ready")
            buf.in_flight.discard(seg_idx)
            buf.cv_buffer_low.notify_all()
```

**StreamingPackedSampler**（追加到 `packing.py`，继承 `PackedFileSampler`）：
```python
class StreamingPackedSampler(PackedFileSampler):
    def __init__(self, buf: StreamBuffer, max_len: int, min_seg_len: int,
                 ttt_pad: int, rng: random.Random):
        self.buf = buf
        self.max_len = max_len
        self.min_seg_len = min_seg_len
        self.ttt_pad = ttt_pad
        self.rng = rng
        self._pool: list[tuple[Path, int]] = []
        self._refill_pool()

    def _refill_pool(self):
        """Wait until min_buffered segments ready, then absorb their files."""
        with self.buf.lock:
            while len(self.buf.ready) < self.buf.min_buffered and \
                  (self.buf.queue or self.buf.in_flight):
                self.buf.cv_buffer_low.wait(timeout=30.0)
            # absorb every ready segment's still-unconsumed files
            pool = []
            for seg in self.buf.ready.values():
                for p in seg.files:
                    if p.name in seg.consumed: continue
                    n = seg.file_lengths.get(p.name, 0)
                    if n >= self.min_seg_len: pool.append((p, n))
        self.rng.shuffle(pool); pool.sort(key=lambda x: -x[1])
        self._pool = pool

    def next_bin(self) -> list[Path]:
        if not self._pool: self._refill_pool()
        if not self._pool:  # exhausted
            raise StopIteration
        bin_files = super().next_bin.__wrapped__(self) if False else self._ffd_one()
        # mark consumed + maybe rmtree segment
        self._mark_consumed(bin_files)
        return bin_files

    def _ffd_one(self):
        # same body as PackedFileSampler.next_bin but no _reset_pool branch
        ...

    def _mark_consumed(self, files: list[Path]):
        with self.buf.lock:
            for p in files:
                # parse seg_idx from filename prefix "segNNNNNN_"
                seg_idx = int(p.name[3:9])
                seg = self.buf.ready.get(seg_idx)
                if seg is None: continue
                seg.consumed.add(p.name)
                if len(seg.consumed) >= len(seg.files):
                    shutil.rmtree(seg.local_dir, ignore_errors=True)
                    self.buf.ready.pop(seg_idx)
                    self.buf.done_count += 1
                    self.buf.cv_buffer_full.notify_all()
```

### D.2 train.py 改动（~30 行）

- `argparse` 加 `--streaming`, `--bos_prefix`, `--stream_workers=8`, `--stream_buffer_segments=12`, `--stream_min_buffered=3`, `--stream_user_4813494d=/user_4813494d/data/eagle_stage/stream`, `--total_segments=4688`。
- 第 785 行附近分叉：
  ```python
  if args.streaming:
      buf = StreamBuffer(args.total_segments, args.stream_buffer_segments,
                         args.stream_min_buffered, Path(args.stream_user_4813494d), rng)
      workers = [threading.Thread(target=_download_worker,
                                  args=(buf, args.bos_prefix, i), daemon=True)
                 for i in range(args.stream_workers)]
      for w in workers: w.start()
      pack_sampler = StreamingPackedSampler(buf, args.seq_len, PACK_MIN_SEG,
                                            TTT_STEPS, rng)
  else:
      files = sorted(data_dir.glob("*.pt"))  # existing path
      ...
  ```
- 训练主循环里包 `try: ... except StopIteration: break`（流式末尾自然终止）。
- 写 `train_log.jsonl` 时多记 `stream_buffered=len(buf.ready)`、`stream_done=buf.done_count`，便于诊断。

### D.3 关键参数论证

- **buffer HWM=12 segments** ≈ 12 × 768 MiB = 9 GB on disk，远小于 50 GB 上限。
- **LWM=3 segments** ≈ 192 file = 46 s 训练消耗（4.13 file/s）— 比 single `bcecmd sync` 启动延迟（~4 s × 12 MiB × 64 file ÷ 106 MB/s ≈ 7-10 s/segment）大 4×，不会 starve。
- **8 workers @ 106 MB/s 饱和**：8.83 file/s 下载 vs 4.13 file/s 训练，2.1× over-supply → HWM 会先到，workers 自然 throttle。
- **shuffle 正确性**：启动一次性 `rng.sample(range(4688))` 决定 segment 顺序，单 epoch 不重复；segment 内 64 file 由 sampler `self.rng.shuffle(pool)` 二次打散。

## 块 E — 测试三连 (3h)

### E.1 Smoke train（45 min）
- **入口**：`bash /user_4813494d/openbmb/eagle/bin/train.sh --max_steps 50`（新增 max_steps 旁路）。数据走静态 `/user_4813494d/data/eagle_stage/main`（先 symlink 一个 256 file 子集到 `main_smoke/`，避免动 BOS）。
- **通过标准**：(a) 50 step 不 OOM；(b) step time ≤ 12 s（TTT=7 baseline 11.5 s）；(c) `train_log.jsonl` 最后 10 step 平均 loss < 第一个 10 step 平均 loss（粗单调）；(d) `nvidia-smi` 峰值 < 84 GB。
- **失败排查**：OOM → 验证 GRAD_CHECKPOINT 状态、BS 是否被环境变量改写；loss 不降 → 打印 `init_mlp_from_target` 后的 fc.weight.norm（应 > 0），打印 vocab cache 命中数；step time 飙高 → `AsyncPrefetcher` 是否启动（log 'prefetcher started'）。

### E.2 Few-samples overfit（30 min）
- **入口**：`python -m eagle.training.sala_draft.train --data_dir /user_4813494d/data/eagle_stage/overfit4 --max_steps 200 --seq_len 2048 --no_eval --lr 5e-4`（用现有的 `overfit_train4_nvfp4` symlink 过去）。
- **通过标准**：(a) step 200 时 `top1_acc_step0` > 0.95（4 file 高 LR 必须过拟合）；(b) loss 200 step 后单调下降 ≥ 10×。
- **失败排查**：acc 卡在 ~0 → vocab mapping (`d2t/t2d`) 没 build 或 cache 路径不对；acc 上升但 plateau ~0.3 → `assistant_mask` tail masking 在 packed 模式下错误；loss NaN → FP4 quant unfreeze 时机不对，验证 `fp4_quant_freeze` 在 forward 前调用。

### E.3 Streaming profile（1.5h）
- **入口**：`python -m eagle.training.sala_draft.train --streaming --bos_prefix bos://anp3-common-model/vista/eagle3_data/v3mix_300k_det_len2048_topk256_aux1-10-22 --total_segments 8 --stream_buffer_segments 3 --stream_min_buffered 2 --stream_workers 4 --max_steps 50 --ttt_steps 3`（TTT=3 baseline 4.604 s/step，与现有 profile 可比）。
- **通过标准**：(a) step time mean < 5.0 s（streaming overhead < 9%）；(b) `stream_buffered` 在 [2, 3] 区间稳定；(c) 训练结束时 `/user_4813494d/data/eagle_stage/stream/` 为空（所有 segment 被 rmtree）；(d) 无 `cv_buffer_low` timeout warn。
- **失败排查**：step time > 5 s 且 `stream_buffered` 常 < 2 → downloader 慢或 indexing 串行成瓶颈，把 `torch.load` 移到独立 indexing thread；step time 正常但 disk 涨 → `_mark_consumed` 没 trigger rmtree（检查 seg_idx 解析的 filename 前缀）；BOS 401/403 → bcecmd ak/sk 配置；磁盘满 → HWM 配置过高或 train 比预期慢。
